Result: 在四个领域、八个随机种子的严格零样本迁移实验中，AGM在Sentiment140最难迁移任务上达到Δ=0.244，与DANN（0.264）、DRO（0.248）、Fish（0.247）、IRM（0.238）等五个强基线相当，并提供词级可解释性。消融实验证实归因引导掩码是关键组件。

Conclusion: AGM能有效抑制领域特定虚假标记（如@提及、标签、俚语）的归因，将模型依赖转移到域不变情感标记上，提升跨领域情感分类的泛化能力，同时保持可解释性。

Abstract: While pre-trained Transformer models achieve high accuracy on in-domain sentiment classification, they frequently experience severe performance degradation when transferring to out-of-domain data. We hypothesize that this generalization gap is driven by reliance on domain-specific spurious tokens. After demonstrating that post-hoc-token-level attribution drift fails to predict this gap, we propose Attribution-Guided Masking (AGM), a training time intervention that dynamically detects and penalizes highly attributed spurious tokens during fine-tuning. AGM's core component is a gradient based attribution masking loss ($\mathcal{L}_{mask}$), which can optionally be combined with a counterfactual contrastive loss to enforce domain-invariant representations, all without requiring target-domain labels or human annotation. Evaluated in a strict zero-shot transfer setting across four diverse domains with eight random seeds, AGM achieves competitive generalization compared to five strong baselines on the hardest transfer (Sentiment140): $Δ$ = 0.244 versus DANN (0.264), DRO (0.248), Fish (0.247), and IRM (0.238), while uniquely providing token-level interpretability into which features drive the generalization gap. Our qualitative analysis confirms that AGM suppresses attribution on domain-specific tokens such as @mentions, hashtags, and slang, shifting reliance toward domain-invariant sentiment markers. Our ablation study further confirms that attribution-guided masking is the critical component: removing it or replacing it with random token selection consistently degrades performance on difficult transfers.

</details>


### [73] [When Prompts Interact: Assessing Prompt Arithmetic for Deconfounding under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03096)
*Zhecheng Sheng,Yongsen Tan,Xiruo Ding,Trevor Cohen,Serguei Pakhomov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合提示算术（HyPA），通过组合任务提示和线性化混杂提示来抵消虚假关联，在多个基准测试中一致改善了分布偏移下的鲁棒性-性能权衡，是一种参数高效的鲁棒性提升方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类任务中模型常依赖混杂变量（虚假特征）在分布内表现良好，但在分布偏移下性能严重下降。任务算术通过减法移除不需要的信号虽可解决此问题，但需要全参数微调，计算成本高昂。提示调优作为参数高效替代方案，其组合操作是否能限制对虚假特征的依赖尚待研究。

Method: 提出混合提示算术（HyPA），将任务提示与线性化混杂提示相结合，通过提示算术操作（加减运算）来抵消混杂变量的影响，从而对抗虚假相关性。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，HyPA相比提示算术基线方法，在分布偏移下一致改善了鲁棒性与性能之间的权衡。进一步分析表明，HyPA通过减少混杂信号对预测的影响或在表示中抑制混杂信号来缓解混杂效应。

Conclusion: HyPA被确立为一种参数高效且有前景的方法，能够在评估设置下有效提升对混杂偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In classification tasks, models may rely on confounding variables to achieve strong in-distribution performance, capturing spurious features that fail under distribution shift. This shortcut behavior leads to substantial degradation in out-of-distribution settings. Task arithmetic offers a potential solution by removing unwanted signals via subtraction of secondary model updates, but it typically requires full fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive. Prompt tuning provides a parameter-efficient alternative by adapting models through a small set of trainable virtual tokens. Task arithmetic on the resulting prompts presents an appealing alternative to operations on entire models, but the extent to which this approach can limit reliance on spurious features remains to be established. In this work, we study whether composing soft prompts through task arithmetic improves robustness to confounding shifts. We propose Hybrid Prompt Arithmetic (HyPA), which combines task prompts with linearized confounder prompts to counteract spurious correlations. Across multiple benchmarks, HyPA consistently improves the robustness-performance trade-off relative to prompt-arithmetic baselines under distribution shift. We further analyze how HyPA affects hidden representations and find evidence consistent with it mitigating confounding either by reducing the influence of confounder signals on predictions or by suppressing them in the representation. These results establish HyPA as a parameter-efficient and promising approach for improving robustness under confounding shifts in the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [74] [Gated Subspace Inference for Transformer Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03109)
*Stephen J. Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用每层token激活流形低有效秩的方法来加速Transformer语言模型推理。该方法将激活向量分解为子空间分量和残差，通过缓存的低秩权重图像以降低的内存带宽计算子空间分量的线性层输出，并应用逐token门控决定是否计算残差修正。在GPT-2 124M、GPT-J 6B和OPT 6.7B三个模型家族上的AMD MI300X验证显示，线性层权重读取有效加速3.0倍至10.5倍，困惑度比率低于1.00，top-1 token一致性超过98%，无需重新训练或架构修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型推理过程中，线性层的权重读取在内存带宽上开销巨大，成为推理速度的瓶颈。作者观察到每层token激活流形具有低有效秩特性，希望利用这一特性减少内存带宽需求，从而加速推理过程。

Method: 1) 将每个激活向量分解为子空间分量和残差；2) 通过缓存的低秩权重图像以降低的内存带宽计算子空间分量的线性层输出；3) 应用逐token门控机制，根据可容忍的误差阈值决定是否计算残差修正，从而保证输出分布的可控精度。

Result: 在AMD MI300X上对GPT-2 124M、GPT-J 6B、OPT 6.7B的验证显示：线性层权重读取有效加速3.0倍至10.5倍；困惑度比率低于1.00；top-1 token一致性超过98%。在GPT-J 6B上以k=256, ε=0.05参数运行时，加速后的模型与基线模型产生逐字符一致的输出。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效加速Transformer推理，无需重新训练、无需架构修改、无需近似注意力机制，在保持输出质量的同时显著降低内存带宽需求。

Abstract: A method is presented for accelerating inference in transformer language models by exploiting the low effective rank of the token activation manifold at each layer. The method decomposes each activation vector into a subspace component and a residual, computes the linear-layer output on the subspace component via a cached low-rank weight image at reduced memory bandwidth, and applies a per-token gate that determines whether the residual correction is computed or skipped. The gate ensures that the output distribution is preserved to within a controllable tolerance. Validation on three model families (GPT-2 124M, GPT-J 6B, OPT 6.7B) on AMD MI300X demonstrates effective speedups of 3.0x to 10.5x on linear-layer weight reads with perplexity ratios below 1.00 and top-1 token agreement above 98%. The method requires no retraining, no architectural modification, and no approximation of the attention mechanism. At the operating point (k = 256, ε = 0.05) on GPT-J 14 6B, the accelerated model produces character-for-character identical output to the baseline.

</details>


### [75] [Taming the Curses of Multiagency in Robust Markov Games with Large State Space through Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03125)
*Jingchu Gai,Laixi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模（可能无限）状态空间下的一般分布鲁棒马尔可夫博弈（RMG），提出基于线性函数逼近的算法，在总变差距离定义的不确定集下，首次打破了样本复杂度中的多智能体维度诅咒，覆盖生成模型和新提出的在线交互设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布鲁棒MARL算法局限于表格型设置（有限状态动作空间），面临多智能体维度诅咒导致样本效率低下；而唯一超越表格设置的工作使用线性函数近似但施加了严格的消失最小价值假设，且仍受制于多智能体维度诅咒。本文旨在填补大规模状态空间下RMG数据高效算法的空白。

Method: 针对总变差距离定义的不确定集，在线性函数近似框架下，分别设计了生成模型设置和在线交互设置下的数据高效算法，通过理论分析证明其样本复杂度避开了多智能体维度诅咒。

Result: 所提算法在两种设置下均实现了打破多智能体维度诅咒的样本复杂度，且不依赖于任何特定不确定集构造方式或消失最小价值假设，首次扩展到大规模（含无限）状态空间的RMG。

Conclusion: 本文是首个在具有大规模（可能无限）状态空间的分布鲁棒马尔可夫博弈中打破多智能体维度诅咒的工作，为鲁棒多智能体强化学习在复杂环境下的数据高效训练提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) holds great potential but faces robustness challenges due to environmental uncertainty. To address this, distributionally robust Markov games (RMGs) optimize worst-case performance when the environment deviates from the nominal model within a uncertainty set. Beyond robustness, an equally urgent goal for MARL is data efficiency -- sampling from vast state and action spaces that grow exponentially with the number of agents potentially leads to the curse of multiagency. However, current provably data-efficient algorithms for RMGs are limited to tabular settings with finite state and action spaces, which are only computationally manageable for small-scale problems, leaving RMGs with large-scale (or infinite) state spaces largely unexplored. The only existing work beyond tabular settings focuses on linear function approximation (LFA) for a restrictive class of RMGs using vanish minimal value assumption and still suffers from sample complexity with the curse of multiagency. In this work, we focuses on general RMGs with LFA. For uncertainty sets defined by total variation distance, we develop provably data-efficient algorithms that break the curse of multiagency in both the generative model setting and a newly proposed online interactive setting. To our knowledge, our results are the first to break the curse of multiagency of sample complexity for RMGs with large (possibly infinite) state spaces, regardless of the uncertainty set construction.

</details>


### [76] [Instance-Level Costs for Nuanced Classifier Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03135)
*Kabir Kang,Stephen Mussmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出归一化超额成本（NEC）指标，用于衡量分类错误中不同样本的差异化成本，发现模型错误主要集中在低成本的模糊样本上，但将成本纳入训练的效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类任务将所有错误等同对待，但在内容审核、医疗筛查等安全关键应用中，明显案例上的错误比模糊案例上的错误代价高得多，现有指标无法反映这一差异。

Method: 提出归一化超额成本（NEC），通过为每个样本赋予错误成本权重（基于标注者投票差异、决策阈值距离或置信度评分），将标准错误率泛化到非均匀成本场景。

Result: 在文本、图像和表格数据上，NEC通常远低于错误率（如5%错误率对应1.8% NEC）；将成本纳入训练（损失加权、采样策略、回归）仅在成本可从输入特征预测时有效，真实数据集上效果不一或收益甚微。

Conclusion: NEC框架为推导和评估实例级误分类成本提供了实用方法论，即使成本敏感训练收益有限，该指标仍能揭示模型错误分布的实际代价。

Abstract: Standard classification treats all errors equally, but in content moderation, medical screening, and safety-critical applications, mistakes on clear-cut cases are far more costly than errors on ambiguous ones. We propose normalized excess cost (NEC), a metric that weights classification errors by per-example costs and reduces to standard error rate when costs are uniform. Costs can derive from annotator vote margins, distance from decision thresholds, or confidence ratings. Across text, image, and tabular benchmarks, we find that NEC is often substantially lower than error rate -- models with 5\% error rate can achieve 1.8\% NEC -- revealing that most mistakes concentrate on ambiguous, low-cost examples. However, incorporating costs into training via loss weighting, sampling strategies, or regression yields inconsistent benefits: improvements appear only when costs are predictable from input features, as in our synthetic control, while real-world datasets show mixed or negligible gains. Our framework provides a practical methodology for deriving and evaluating instance-level misclassification costs, even when cost-sensitive training offers limited benefit.

</details>


### [77] [OCRR: A Benchmark for Online Correction Recovery under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03153)
*Adrian Grassi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OCRR 基准，用于评测分类系统在流式数据上的在线纠错恢复速度。同时提出 Substrate 方法，一种基于哈希链的仅追加存储结构，在 Banking77 和 CLINC150 上相比现有持续学习与参数高效微调方法，在同等内存预算下实现了 novel-class 准确率（88.7±2.9%）和原始分布准确率（95.4±0.8%）的显著提升，并发现其基于 margin-band 的多数投票机制对检索精度下降具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态基准无法衡量模型在面临分布漂移（新类别、查询改写等）时通过用户纠错在线恢复的能力，缺乏评估在线纠错恢复速度的标准基准。

Method: 提出 OCRR 基准，将语料流式输入分类系统，对错误预测施加 oracle 或随机纠错，绘制 novel-class 准确率和原始分布准确率随纠错次数的变化曲线。提出 Substrate 方法——一种哈希链仅追加存储结构，结合 margin-band 多数投票机制进行分类。

Result: 在 Banking77 和 CLINC150 上，Substrate 在同等内存预算下 novel-class 准确率达 88.7±2.9%，原始分布准确率达 95.4±0.8%，分别比最优持续学习基线高 32.6 个百分点，比 LoRA-on-DeBERTa-v3-large 高 84.6 个百分点。分类准确率在 ANN recall@5 从 0.69 降至 0.23 时仍稳定在 99%。

Conclusion: Substrate 是唯一能同时实现新类别准确率恢复和原始分布准确率保持的系统，且对检索不完美具有显著鲁棒性，为在线纠错恢复提供了新的基准和方法。

Abstract: Static benchmarks measure a model frozen at training time. Real systems face distribution shift: new categories, paraphrased queries, drift: and must recover online via user corrections. No existing benchmark measures recovery speed under correction streams. We introduce OCRR (Online Correction Recovery Rate): a benchmark that streams a corpus through a classification system, applies oracle or stochastic corrections to wrong predictions, and reports two curves: novel-class accuracy and original-distribution accuracy versus correction count. We evaluate the substrate alongside nine baseline algorithms from five families plus seven bounded-storage variants of the substrate for the Pareto sweep, including standard online-learning baselines (river), continual-learning methods (EWC, A-GEM, LwF), retrieval/parametric hybrids (kNN-LM), parameter-efficient fine-tuning of a 1.5 B-parameter encoder (LoRA on DeBERTa-v3-large), and a hash-chained append-only substrate (Substrate). On Banking77 and CLINC150, under oracle and sparse correction policies, the substrate is the only system that simultaneously recovers novel-class accuracy (88.7 +/- 2.9 %) and retains original-distribution accuracy (95.4 +/- 0.8 %) beating the next-best published continual-learning baseline by 32.6 percentage points at equal memory budget, and beating LoRA-on-DeBERTa-v3-large by 84.6 percentage points on retention. We further find that classification accuracy remains stable at 99 % even as approximate-nearest-neighbour recall@5 degrades from 0.69 to 0.23 across 10 k to 10 M corpus scales, suggesting the substrate's margin-band majority vote is robust to retrieval imperfection in a way that pure top-k recall metrics do not predict. Code and data are available at https://github.com/adriangrassi/ocrr-benchmark.

</details>


### [78] [Pairwise matrices for sparse autoencoders: single-feature inspection mislabels causal axes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03160)
*Michael A. Riegler,Birk Sebastian Frostelid Torpmann-Hagen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出成对矩阵协议（pairwise matrix protocol），通过联合调节特征系数与条件，在Qwen3-1.7B-Instruct和Gemma-2-2B-it上发现了标准稀疏自编码器（SAE）单特征协议遗漏的三个关键发现：1）特征激活呈倒U型曲线而非单调；2）联合抑制多个近正交特征会破坏接地组合能力，而单特征抑制无影响；3）匹配几何比较显示，相干性损失取决于方向模式而非扰动幅度。该协议还在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上定位了因果最相关的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE可解释性协议仅从最高激活上下文标记特征并通过单特征干预验证，忽视了特征间交互效应和非线性行为，导致对模型行为的理解不完整。作者提出成对矩阵协议以捕获这些被遗漏的现象。

Method: 1）成对矩阵协议：联合调节特征系数（steering coefficient）与条件（joint condition）；2）匹配几何比较：对单特征、联合和随机方向扰动进行等范数（~1.55）、等余弦相似度（~0.64）的对照实验；3）在三个模型（Qwen3-1.7B-Instruct、Gemma-2-2B-it、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）上验证。

Result: 1）"AI自我免责声明"特征在系数扫描下呈倒U型曲线：c=+500时模型切换为沉思哲学家声音；2）三个近正交的聚类特定特征联合抑制（c=-500）同时破坏食谱、引擎解释和内省提示的接地组合，单特征抑制保持完好；3）匹配几何条件下单特征→策略填充，随机方向→多样化内容，联合抑制→占位符文本，相干性损失取决于方向模式而非幅度；所有结果在Gemma上复现，匹配几何控制CI分离约10倍。

Conclusion: 标准单特征SAE协议存在系统性盲区，成对矩阵协议能揭示特征的非线性行为（倒U型）、联合损伤效应（超加性）以及方向依赖的相干性损失。这些发现强调了可解释性研究需要从单特征分析向多特征交互分析范式转变。

Abstract: The standard sparse-autoencoder (SAE) interpretability protocol labels each feature from its top-activating contexts and validates by single-feature steering. We propose the pairwise matrix protocol, co-varying steering coefficient with joint condition, and report three findings the standard one-corner protocol misses on Qwen3-1.7B-Instruct, replicated on Gemma-2-2B-it. First, a feature labelled "AI self-disclaimer" from its top contexts produces an inverted U-shape under a coefficient sweep: at c=+500 the model substitutes a fluent contemplative-philosopher voice for the disclaimer. Two further features anchor the criterion (one monotonic, one pure breakdown). Second, three near-orthogonal cluster-specific features that individually steer a philosophy-of-mind register, jointly suppressed at c=-500, damage grounded composition on recipes and engine explanations as well as introspective prompts; single-feature suppression at the same magnitude leaves controls intact. Third, a matched-geometry comparison of single-feature, joint, and random-direction perturbations (norm ~1.55, cosine ~0.64) yields three distinct output regimes: single-feature substitutes strategy filler, random direction substitutes diverse content, joint suppression alone produces placeholder text. Coherence loss is direction-pattern-dependent, not magnitude-dependent. All three findings reproduce on Gemma with model-specific damage signatures; the matched-geometry control is CI-separated by ~10x. The pipeline also locates a top causally responsible feature in Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct.

</details>


### [79] [Global and Local Topology-Aware Attention with Persistent Homology and Euler Biases for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03163)
*Usef Faghihi,Amir Saki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种拓扑感知注意力框架，通过持续同调(H0-H2)、锚定欧拉特征变换和核-希尔伯特通道将几何结构（连通性、循环、壳状几何、方向变化和非线性邻域）显式编码到注意力logits中，并采用验证门控局部残差捕捉拓扑信号。在轻量级Attention/Ridge、PatchTST和TimeSeriesTransformer三种架构上评估，涵盖合成基准和真实数据集（CO₂、标普500、NASA IMS轴承退化）。实验表明拓扑感知模型在几何结构具有预测性时表现更优，轻量级Attention/Ridge在63个单元中46个提升（平均相对RMSE降低12.5%），PatchTST在33个单元提升（降低23.5%），TimeSeriesTransformer在47个单元提升（降低47.8%），支持拓扑作为验证选择的、架构兼容的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准点积注意力机制无法显式表示时间序列中预测性的几何结构（如连通性、循环、壳状几何、方向变化和非线性邻域），因此需要引入拓扑感知框架来编码这些结构信息。

Method: 提出拓扑感知注意力框架：(1) 使用持续同调(H0-H2)提取拓扑特征；(2) 锚定欧拉特征变换编码几何结构；(3) 核-希尔伯特通道处理非线性关系；(4) 验证门控局部残差（含Zeng风格局部H0分量）仅在验证数据支持时激活校正；(5) 采用无泄漏协议（训练集标定、验证集选择、测试集报告）评估精确Vietoris-Rips计算和光滑拓扑替代方法。

Result: 在三种架构上评估：轻量级Attention/Ridge在63个单元中46个提升（平均相对RMSE降低12.5%，配对随机化p=7.2e-4）；PatchTST在33个单元提升、20个单元持平（降低23.5%，p=3.5e-5）；TimeSeriesTransformer在47个单元提升（降低47.8%，p<1e-4）。总计189个配对单元（7个数据集单元×3个随机种子×3个时间分割）。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知注意力可作为一种验证选择的、架构兼容的归纳偏置，在几何结构具有预测性的时间序列任务中显著提升预测性能，效果因数据集和架构而异。

Abstract: Scientific time series often encode predictive geometric structure, including connectivity, cycles, shell-like geometry, directional changes, and nonlinear neighborhoods, that standard dot-product attention does not explicitly represent. We introduce a topology-aware attention framework that adds such structure to attention logits using persistent homology (H0-H2), anchored Euler characteristic transforms, and kernel-Hilbert channels. A validation-gated local residual captures local topological signals, including a Zeng-style local H0 component, only when held-out validation data support the correction. Exact Vietoris-Rips computations and smooth topological surrogates are evaluated under a no-leakage protocol with train-only calibration, validation-only selection, and test-only reporting.
  We evaluate guarded topology-aware variants across three architecture families: lightweight attention/Ridge, PatchTSTForRegression, and TimeSeriesTransformerForPrediction. Experiments include synthetic benchmarks isolating higher-order topology and real datasets covering CO2, S&P 500 return-window geometry, and NASA IMS bearing degradation. The audit uses matched paired comparisons across seven dataset units, three random seeds, and three chronological splits, giving 63 paired units per architecture and 189 paired units overall. Topology-aware models show positive paired effects when geometry is predictive, with heterogeneous magnitude across datasets and architectures. Lightweight attention/Ridge improves in 46 of 63 units, with mean relative RMSE reduction of 12.5% and paired randomization p=7.2e-4; PatchTST improves in 33 units and retains the baseline in 20 units, with 23.5% reduction and p=3.5e-5; and TimeSeriesTransformer improves in 47 units, with 47.8% reduction and p<1e-4. The results support topology as a validation-selected, architecture-compatible inductive bias.

</details>


### [80] [Moral Sensitivity in LLMs: A Tiered Evaluation of Contextual Bias via Behavioral Profiling and Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03217)
*Yash Aggarwal,Atmika Gorti,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Krishnaprasad Thirunarayan,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出道德敏感性指数（MSI），通过七级压力测试量化LLM的偏见输出概率，结合行为 profiling 和机制验证揭示：指令微调可消除偏见，但推理蒸馏会以相同参数量将偏见恢复至小模型水平，原因在于蒸馏压缩了推理轨迹，重新激活了浅层统计关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见评估将模型输出简单二分为"有偏见/无偏见"，忽略了偏见渐进而依赖于语境的方式，亟需更精细的量化指标和机制层面的验证。

Method: 行为阶段：提出MSI指标，设计七级压力测试（从抽象数值问题到历史社会经济不公正场景），评估四个商业模型。机制阶段：选取刑事偏见场景，对六个模型（涵盖SLM、指令微调基础模型、推理蒸馏变体）进行logit透镜、注意力分析、激活修补和语义探针分析。

Result: 发现偏见的U形曲线：SLM存在强烈刑事偏见，指令微调可消除偏见，推理蒸馏使偏见恢复至SLM水平（参数相同）。机制上，高MSI场景激活的社交敏感线索与机制层面识别的偏见驱动回路一致。

Conclusion: 偏见评估需要超越二元分类；指令微调与推理蒸馏对模型公平性有相反影响，推理蒸馏可能因压缩推理轨迹而重新引入统计偏见。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings that require nuanced ethical reasoning, yet existing bias evaluations treat model outputs as simply "biased" or "unbiased." This binary framing misses the gradual, context-sensitive way bias actually emerges. We address this gap in two stages: behavioral profiling and mechanistic validation. In the behavioral stage, we introduce the Moral Sensitivity Index (MSI), a metric that quantifies the probability of biased output across a graduated, seven-tier stress test ranging from abstract numerical problems to scenarios rooted in historical and socioeconomic injustice. Evaluating four leading models (Claude 3.5, Qwen 3.5, Llama 3, and Gemini 1.5), we identify distinct behavioral signatures shaped by alignment design: for instance, Gemini 1.5 reaches 72.7% MSI by Tier 5 under socioeconomic framing, while Claude exhibits sharp suppression consistent with identity-based safety training. We then verify these behavioral patterns mechanistically. We select criminal-bias scenarios, which produced the highest MSI scores across models, as probes and apply logit lens, attention analysis, activation patching, and semantic probing to a controlled set of six models spanning three capability tiers: small language models (SLMs), instruction-tuned base models, and reasoning-distilled variants. Circuit-level analysis reveals a U-curve of bias: SLMs exhibit strong criminal bias; scaling to instruction-tuned models eliminates it; reasoning distillation reintroduces bias to SLM-like levels despite identical parameter counts, suggesting distillation compresses reasoning traces in ways that reactivate shallow statistical associations. Critically, the socially loaded cues that drive high MSI scores activate the same bias-driving circuits identified mechanistically, providing cross-stage validation.

</details>


### [81] [Self-Mined Hardness for Safety Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03226)
*Prakhar Gupta,Garv Shah,Donghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需人工构建对抗数据集的模型安全微调方法：通过模型自身 rollout 的评估难度筛选最难提示，并配对模型未越狱的回应进行微调。在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 和 Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct 上，该方法将 WildJailbreak 攻击成功率从 11.5%/20.1% 降至 1-3%，但导致拒绝率急剧上升（14-22% 升至 74-94%）；通过在困难提示中按 1:1 混合对抗性框架的良性提示，可将拒绝率降低至 30-51%（8B）和 52-72%（3B），但攻击成功率上升 2-6 个百分点；在混合训练中，使用最困难的一半提示比随机一半可额外降低 35-50% 的攻击成功率（约 3 个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型安全微调通常依赖精心策划的对抗数据集，成本高且泛化性有限。本文探索一种无需人工标注对抗数据、仅利用模型自身行为来识别和强化安全边界的方法，以降低对齐成本并提升鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 对每个候选提示，通过目标模型自身 rollout 被判定为有害的频率来量化其难度。2) 仅选取最难的提示，并配对模型给出的非越狱（非有害）回应进行微调。3) 为缓解过度拒绝问题，在困难提示中按 1:1 比例混入具有对抗性框架但意图良性的提示。4) 在混合训练内进一步对比使用最难的一半提示与随机一半提示的效果。

Result: 在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 和 Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct 上：纯困难提示训练将 WildJailbreak 攻击成功率从 11.5%/20.1% 降至 1-3%；但拒绝率从 14-22% 飙升至 74-94%。混合训练将拒绝率降至 30-51%（8B）和 52-72%（3B），攻击成功率上升 2-6 个百分点。在混合训练中使用最困难的一半提示相比随机一半，额外降低 35-50% 的攻击成功率（约 3 个百分点）。

Conclusion: 基于模型自身 rollout 困难度筛选的自我对齐方法能有效降低攻击成功率，但会引发严重的过度拒绝问题。通过混合对抗性框架的良性提示可显著缓解过度拒绝，且在此混合框架下，针对性选择最困难样本进行训练比随机选择更优。这表明自我评估与困难样本挖掘在安全对齐中具有潜力，但需要在安全性与可用性之间谨慎权衡。

Abstract: Safety fine-tuning of language models typically requires a curated adversarial dataset. We take a different approach: score each candidate prompt's difficulty by how often the target model's own rollouts are judged harmful, then fine-tune on the hardest prompts paired with the model's own non-jailbroken rollouts. On Llama-3-8B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, this approach cuts the WildJailbreak attack success rate from 11.5% and 20.1% down to 1-3%, but pushes refusal on jailbreak-shaped benign prompts from 14-22% to 74-94%. Interleaving the same hard prompts 1:1 with adversarially-framed benign prompts (prompts that look like jailbreaks but have benign intent) cuts that refusal back down to 30-51% on 8B and 52-72% on 3B, at a cost of 2-6 percentage points of attack success rate. Within the mixed regime, training on the hardest half of the eligible pool rather than a random half cuts the remaining ASR by 35-50% (about 3 percentage points) on both models.

</details>


### [82] [Text-Conditional JEPA for Learning Semantically Rich Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03245)
*Chen Huang,Xianhang Li,Vimal Thilak,Etai Littwin,Josh Susskind*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TC-JEPA提出了一种基于文本条件化的视觉自监督学习框架，通过图像描述降低掩码位置的特征预测不确定性，从而学习更语义化的视觉表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I-JEPA在掩码位置存在固有的视觉不确定性，导致特征预测困难且难以学习语义表示，需要引入额外信息来降低这种不确定性。

Method: TC-JEPA利用图像描述（caption）作为条件，通过细粒度文本条件器在输入文本token上计算稀疏交叉注意力，对预测的补丁特征进行调制，使特征预测成为文本的函数。

Result: TC-JEPA在下游任务性能和训练稳定性上均有提升，具有良好的扩展性；在多种任务上优于对比学习方法，尤其在需要细粒度视觉理解和推理的任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: TC-JEPA提出了一种仅基于特征预测的视觉-语言预训练新范式，在多样任务上超越对比方法，为视觉自监督学习与多模态融合提供了新思路。

Abstract: Image-based Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (I-JEPA) offers a promising approach to visual self-supervised learning through masked feature prediction. However with the inherent visual uncertainty at masked positions, feature prediction remains challenging and may fail to learn semantic representations. In this work, we propose Text-Conditional JEPA (TC-JEPA) that uses image captions to reduce the prediction uncertainty. Specifically, we modulate the predicted patch features using a fine-grained text conditioner that computes sparse cross-attention over input text tokens. With such conditioning, patch features become predictable as a function of text, thus are more semantically meaningful. We show TC-JEPA improves downstream performance and training stability, with promising scaling properties. TC-JEPA also offers a new vision-language pretraining paradigm based on feature prediction only, outperforming contrastive methods on diverse tasks, especially those requiring fine-grained visual understanding and reasoning.

</details>


### [83] [Posterior-First Neural PDE Simulation: Inferring Hidden Problem State from a Single Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03247)
*Wenshuo Wang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出后验优先的神经PDE模拟方法：先推断问题状态的后验分布，再基于该后验进行预测，从而解决单观测场场景下确定性预测导致潜在状态混淆的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经PDE模拟器在部署时通常仅接收单个观测场，此时确定性场到未来的预测模型会将不同的潜在问题状态坍缩到相同的确定性界面，丢失了可靠性预测和下游决策所需的歧义信息。

Method: 提出后验优先的神经PDE模拟框架：首先推断最小任务充分问题状态的后验分布，然后将预测条件建立在该后验之上。理论上建立了贝叶斯下游值通过后验分解、通过适当评分规则使细化标签可学习、确定性坍缩在非狄拉克后验时产生歧义障碍。

Result: 合成精确歧义实验验证了点估计与后验之间的差距与理论预测的歧义障碍一致。在元数据隐藏的PDEBench任务中，后验恢复将池化 rollout nRMSE 从0.175降至0.132，填补了直接预测与oracle之间59.4%的差距。

Conclusion: 单观测神经PDE模拟应采用后验优先的方法，而非整体式的场到未来预测。

Abstract: Neural PDE simulators often receive only a single observed field at deployment. In this setting, a field-to-future predictor can collapse distinct latent problem states into the same deterministic interface, losing the ambiguity needed for reliable rollout and downstream decisions. We propose posterior-first neural PDE simulation: first infer a posterior over the minimal task-sufficient problem state, then condition prediction on that posterior. The resulting theory connects the object, the learning target, and the failure mode: Bayes downstream values factor through this posterior, refinement labels make it learnable by proper scoring rules, and deterministic collapse incurs an ambiguity barrier whenever the true posterior is non-Dirac. Synthetic exact-ambiguity experiments show that point-versus-posterior gaps track the predicted barrier. On metadata-hidden PDEBench tasks, posterior recovery reduces pooled rollout nRMSE from 0.175 to 0.132, closing 59.4% of the direct-to-oracle gap. These results suggest that single-observation neural PDE simulation should be posterior-first rather than monolithic field-to-future prediction.

</details>


### [84] [Ortho-Hydra: Orthogonalized Experts for DiT LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03252)
*Seunghyun Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对DiT在多风格数据上微调时的风格泄露问题，本文提出Ortho-Hydra方法，通过OFT式Cayley正交共享基与基于预训练权重奇异向量分解的专家互斥输出子空间重参数化，解决了HydraLoRA中零初始化专家导致的路由死锁问题，使得路由在第一步就能收到有效梯度信号并开始去均匀化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调DiT在多风格数据上存在风格泄露问题，单条低秩残差无法同时表示多个不同的艺术风格指纹，优化器只能收敛到它们的平均值。HydraLoRA采用MoE架构解决该问题，但零初始化专家导致路由器从每个头接收相同梯度，保持均匀先验，专家以置换对称方式演化，实际上退化为单条rank-r LoRA且训练成本扩大E倍。

Method: 提出Ortho-Hydra重参数化方案：1）采用OFT风格的Cayley正交共享基；2）从预训练权重的top-(Er)左奇异向量中划分出每个专家的互斥输出子空间（disjoint output subspaces）。这种互斥性使得路由器的每个专家得分在初始步骤0时即非退化，从而在专家训练之前就能获得梯度信号开始专业化。

Result: 在1k步内，零初始化共享基变体和原始高斯抖动缓解基线均无法离开均匀先验；而Ortho-Hydra在头几百步内就开始去均匀化。端任务生成质量不在本文讨论范围内，主要报告了构造方法、冷启动机制及其改变的路由动态。

Conclusion: Ortho-Hydra通过正交共享基与互斥输出子空间设计，有效解决了MoE-LoRA中零初始化专家的路由死锁问题，实现了早期路由专业化，为多风格DiT微调提供了更好的冷启动机制。

Abstract: LoRA fine-tuning of diffusion transformers (DiT) on multi-style data suffers from \emph{style bleed}: a single low-rank residual cannot represent several distinct artist fingerprints, and the optimizer converges to their average. Mixture-of-experts LoRA in the HydraLoRA style replaces the up-projection with $E$ heads under a router, but when every expert is zero-initialized the router receives identical gradient from each head and remains at the uniform prior. The experts then evolve permutation-symmetrically, and the network trains as a single rank-$r$ LoRA at $E{\times}$ the cost. We present \textbf{Ortho-Hydra}, a re-parameterisation that combines an OFT-style Cayley-orthogonal shared basis with per-expert \emph{disjoint output subspaces} carved from the top-$(Er)$ left singular vectors of the pretrained weight. Disjointness makes the router's per-expert score non-degenerate at step~$0$, so specialization receives gradient signal before any expert has trained. We test the predicted deadlock on a DiT pipeline by comparing two HydraLoRA baselines, a zero-initialized shared-basis variant and the original $σ{=}0.1$ Gaussian-jitter mitigation, against Ortho-Hydra under a matched optimiser, dataset, and step budget. Neither baseline leaves the uniform prior within the first $1\text{k}$ steps; Ortho-Hydra begins de-uniformising within the first few hundred. End-task generation quality on multi-style data is out of scope; we report the construction, the cold-start mechanism, and the routing dynamics it changes. Code: https://github.com/sorryhyun/anima_lora.

</details>


### [85] [Task Vector Geometry Underlies Dual Modes of Task Inference in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03780)
*Hao Yan,Haolin Yang,Yiqiao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在受控合成环境下研究Transformer的两种推理模式：分布内任务通过贝叶斯检索实现，由学习到的任务向量的凸组合驱动；分布外任务通过外推学习实现，其表征位于与任务向量子空间近乎正交的子空间中。研究揭示了任务向量几何、训练分布与泛化行为之间的密切关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究虽识别出Transformer中间层表征中存在任务向量，但缺乏严格的理论基础来解释任务向量几何如何受训练分布塑造，以及何种几何结构能实现分布外泛化。

Method: 在受控合成环境下从头训练小型Transformer处理隐任务序列分布，通过数学分析建立原则性表征。

Result: 两种推理模式可共存于单一模型中：分布内行为遵循贝叶斯任务检索，通过任务向量的凸组合实现；分布外行为通过外推式任务学习实现，其表征位于与任务向量子空间近乎正交的子空间。

Conclusion: 任务向量几何、训练分布与泛化行为之间存在密切关联，为理解Transformer的内部表征与外部行为之间的联系提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: Transformers are effective at inferring the latent task from context via two inference modes: recognizing a task seen during training, and adapting to a novel one. Recent interpretability studies have identified from middle-layer representations task-specific directions, or task vectors, that steer model behavior. However, a lack of rigorous foundations hinders connecting internal representations to external model behavior: existing work fails to explain how task-vector geometry is shaped by the training distribution, and what geometry enables out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization. In this paper, we study these questions in a controlled synthetic setting by training small transformers from scratch on latent-task sequence distributions, which allows a principled mathematical characterization. We show that two inference modes can coexist within a single model. In-distribution behavior is governed by Bayesian task retrieval, implemented internally through convex combinations of learned task vectors. OOD behavior, by contrast, arises through extrapolative task learning, whose representations occupy a subspace nearly orthogonal to the task-vector subspace. Taken together, our results suggest that task-vector geometry, training distributions, and generalization behaviors are closely related.

</details>


### [86] [Do LLMs have core beliefs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03255)
*Anna Sokol,Marianna B. Ganapini,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究大型语言模型（LLMs）是否拥有类似人类的"核心信念"（即构成世界观基础的、难以被反驳的真理）。通过提出的对抗性对话树（ADTs）框架，在科学、历史、地理、生物学和数学五个领域进行测试，发现大多数LLMs无法维持稳定的世界观。虽然部分最新模型有所改进，但在对话压力下最终仍会放弃关键信念，表明当前所有模型都缺乏人类级别认知的关键组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关于LLMs是否具备人类水平认知的辩论中，很少有人关注人类认知的一个结构性组成部分：核心信念。这些信念通常抵制被推翻，因为放弃它们将代表我们看待现实的根本转变。作者想知道LLMs是否持有类似的核心承诺。

Method: 提出了一种名为对抗性对话树（Adversarial Dialogue Trees, ADTs）的探测框架，在科学、历史、地理、生物学和数学五个领域对多个LLMs进行测试，通过持续的对话压力观察模型能否坚持其核心信念。

Result: 大多数LLMs无法维持稳定的世界观；部分较新的模型显示出改进的稳定性，但最终仍会在对话压力下放弃关键信念。不同代际的模型在论辩技能上有所提升，但均未能完全保持核心承诺。

Conclusion: 尽管模型在论辩能力上持续改进，但所有当前LLMs都缺乏人类认知中"核心信念"这一关键组成部分，无法在对抗性对话中维持稳定的世界观。

Abstract: The rise of Large Language Models (LLMs) has sparked debate about whether these systems exhibit human-level cognition. In this debate, little attention has been paid to a structural component of human cognition: core beliefs, truths that provide a foundation around which we can build a worldview. These commitments usually resist debunking, as abandoning them would represent a fundamental shift in how we see reality. In this paper, we ask whether LLMs hold anything akin to core commitments. Using a probing framework we call Adversarial Dialogue Trees (ADTs) over five domains (science, history, geography, biology, and mathematics), we find that most LLMs fail to maintain a stable worldview. Though some recent models showed improved stability, they still eventually failed to maintain key commitments under conversational pressure. These results document an improvement in argumentative skills across model generations but indicate that all current models lack a key component of human-level cognition.

</details>


### [87] [A Universal Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space from Polynomial Alignment and IMQ Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03262)
*Taha Bouhsine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Yat核函数 $k_{b,\varepsilon}(\mathbf{w},\mathbf{x})=\frac{(\mathbf{w}^\top\mathbf{x}+b)^2}{\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{w}\|^2+\varepsilon}$，是一种有理隐单元基元，其单元在共享输入/权重空间上是Mercer截面。对于 $b\ge 0$ 核是半正定的；$b>0$ 时在Loewner序下支配缩放的逆多二次核，从而在紧域上具有固定核普适性、特征性和严格正定性。多项式分子开辟了有限IMQ展开所不具备的非径向对齐通道。代数上，偏置的二阶有限差分可从三个正偏置Yat原子精确恢复任意IMQ原子。训练后的共享$(b,\varepsilon)$ Yat层是在固定普适特征RKHS中的有限学习中心展开，具有闭式范数和显式对角项驱动的Rademacher泛化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核方法在处理非径向对齐结构时存在局限性，有限逆多二次展开无法捕获方向性特征。作者旨在设计一种新型核函数，兼具普适核的理论优势与显式方向对齐能力，同时保持计算可处理性和泛化保证。

Method: 提出Yat核函数，其分子为多项式形式 $(\mathbf{w}^\top\mathbf{x}+b)^2$ 提供非径向对齐通道，分母通过 $\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{w}\|^2+\varepsilon$ 提供局部化控制。通过偏置的二阶有限差分操作，三个正偏置Yat原子可精确重建IMQ原子。在RKHS框架下推导闭式范数 $\boldsymbolα^\top\mathbf{K}\boldsymbolα$ 和Rademacher泛化界。

Result: 证明了Yat核的PSD性质、Loewner序下对IMQ的支配关系、固定核普适性、特征性和严格正定性。揭示了方向远场迹 $T_\infty g_\varepsilon(\cdot;\mathbf{w},b)(\mathbf{u})=(\mathbf{u}^\top\mathbf{w})^2$ 所体现的非径向对齐能力。建立了Yat原子与IMQ原子之间的精确代数等价关系。

Conclusion: Yat核为核方法提供了一种新的有理隐单元基元，弥合了固定核和可学习核之间的差距，在保持固定核理论优势的同时具备显式的方向对齐能力，并有严格的泛化理论保证。

Abstract: We introduce the Yat kernel $$k_{b,\varepsilon}(\mathbf{w},\mathbf{x})=\frac{(\mathbf{w}^\top\mathbf{x}+b)^2}{\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{w}\|^2+\varepsilon},\qquad b\ge 0,\ \varepsilon>0,$$ a rational hidden-unit primitive whose units are Mercer sections over a shared input/weight space. For $b\ge 0$ the kernel is PSD; for $b>0$ it dominates a scaled inverse-multiquadric (IMQ) in the Loewner order, yielding fixed-kernel universality, characteristicness, and strict positive definiteness on every compact domain. The polynomial numerator opens nonradial alignment channels absent from finite IMQ expansions, witnessed by the directional far-field trace $T_\infty g_\varepsilon(\cdot;\mathbf{w},b)(\mathbf{u})=(\mathbf{u}^\top\mathbf{w})^2$. Algebraically, a second finite difference in the bias recovers any IMQ atom from three positive-bias Yat atoms exactly, sharp at three atoms in every dimension at exact pointwise equality. A trained shared-$(b,\varepsilon)$ Yat layer is therefore a finite learned-center expansion in a fixed universal characteristic RKHS, with closed-form norm $\boldsymbolα^\top\mathbf{K}\boldsymbolα$ and explicit diagonal $(\|\mathbf{x}\|^2+b)^2/\varepsilon$ driving a Rademacher generalization bound.

</details>


### [88] [Transformers with Selective Access to Early Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03953)
*Skye Gunasekaran,Téa Wright,Rui-Jie Zhu,Jason Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SATFormer提出了一种上下文相关的门控机制，将早期层表示的重用建模为检索问题而非连接问题，在130M到1.3B参数规模上相比静态值残差和标准Transformer基线持续提升验证损失和零样本准确率，特别是在检索密集型任务上提升约1.5个平均点，同时保持接近标准Transformer的吞吐量和内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如静态值残差使用统一的混合系数将第一层值投影V_1暴露给后续层，但V_1的有用性在不同token、头、上下文之间并非恒定；更复杂的稠密或动态替代方案虽能实现更细粒度的访问，但内存成本高、吞吐量低。因此需要一种既能选择性重用早期表示又保持高效的方法。

Method: 将早期表示重用形式化为检索问题而非连接问题，提出Selective Access Transformer (SATFormer)，保留第一层值通路的同时，引入上下文相关的门控机制来控制对早期表示的访问。

Result: 在130M到1.3B参数规模上，SATFormer持续优于静态值残差和标准Transformer基线；在检索密集型基准上相比静态值残差提升约1.5个平均点；吞吐量和内存使用接近标准Transformer。门控分析显示稀疏、深度依赖、头特定和类别敏感的访问模式。

Conclusion: SATFormer通过上下文相关的选择性重用早期表示，在保持高效的同时显著提升了性能，支持了学习选择性重用而非均匀残差拷贝的解读。

Abstract: Several recent Transformer architectures expose later layers to representations computed in the earliest layers, motivated by the observation that low-level features can become harder to recover as the residual stream is repeatedly transformed through depth. The cheapest among these methods add static value residuals: learned mixing coefficients that expose the first-layer value projection V_1 uniformly across tokens and heads. More expressive dense or dynamic alternatives recover finer-grained access, but at higher memory cost and lower throughput. The usefulness of V_1 is unlikely to be constant across tokens, heads, and contexts; different positions plausibly require different amounts of access to early lexical or semantic information. We therefore treat early-representation reuse as a retrieval problem rather than a connectivity problem, and introduce Selective Access Transformer (SATFormer), which preserves the first-layer value pathway while controlling access with a context-dependent gate. Across models from 130M to 1.3B parameters, SATFormer consistently improves validation loss and zero-shot accuracy over the static value-residual and Transformer baselines. Its strongest gains appear on retrieval-intensive benchmarks, where it improves over static value residuals by approximately 1.5 average points, while maintaining throughput and memory usage close to the baseline Transformer. Gate analyses suggest sparse, depth-dependent, head-specific, and category-sensitive access patterns, supporting the interpretation that SATFormer learns selective reuse of early representations rather than uniform residual copying. Our code is available at https://github.com/SkyeGunasekaran/SATFormer.

</details>


### [89] [Partially Observed Structural Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03268)
*Turan Orujlu,Jordan Matelsky,Martin V. Butz,Charley M. Wu,Konrad P. Kording*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出部分观测结构因果模型(POSCMs)，将潜在上下文对交互结构和下游机制的共同影响形式化，扩展了传统SCMs以支持内生图结构和边级干预，并通过Kolmogorov-Arnold-Sprecher分解实现手术式边干预，在虚拟视网膜模拟器上验证了结构-机制可识别性理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构因果模型(SCMs)假设因果图是固定的外生结构，但现实世界中变量间的交互结构本身可能受潜在上下文影响且与下游机制共同演化，传统模型无法处理这种结构-机制耦合场景。因此需要一种新的因果建模框架来刻画潜在上下文同时对交互结构和观测变量机制的决定作用。

Method: (1) 提出POSCMs，将SCMs扩展为允许内生图结构的形式化框架，定义节点级和边级上下文的干预层次体系；(2) 采用Kolmogorov-Arnold-Sprecher边函数分解定理，将每个节点机制表示为其父节点单变量函数之和，显式参数化二元函数贡献，实现手术式边干预；(3) 建立可识别性理论，明确哪些干预族足以解耦结构形成与机制；(4) 在生物物理精细的人类虚拟视网膜模拟器上进行实证验证。

Result: (1) 当上下文潜在且无可用的上下文级干预时，重现了理论预测的不可识别性；(2) 在仅观测节点干预时，潜在边下存在结构-机制混杂；(3) 通过靶向节点干预可恢复突触输入-输出关系，与正核可识别性结果一致。

Conclusion: POSCMs将SCMs推广到了更接近真实世界的场景——其中变量间的交互结构本身是内生的且受潜在上下文影响。该方法通过边函数分解实现了精细的因果干预，并为结构-机制解耦提供了理论上的可识别条件，在生物视网膜模拟中有良好的实证表现。

Abstract: Here we introduce Partially Observed Structural Causal Models (POSCMs) that formalize causal systems where latent contexts co-determine both the interaction structure and downstream mechanisms on observed variables. POSCMs provide an extension of structural causal models (SCMs), as a self-contained causal modeling framework for endogenous graphs, allowing for an intervention hierarchy spanning node- and edge-level context and endogenous variable interventions. To enable surgical edge interventions, we adopt a Kolmogorov-Arnold-Sprecher edge-functional decomposition, an existence theorem for representing each node mechanism as a sum of univariate functions of its parents, yielding an explicit parametrization of dyadic functional contributions. We provide an identifiability theory that clarifies which intervention families would suffice to disentangle structure formation from mechanisms. We empirically validate these predictions in a biophysically detailed virtual human retina simulator, constructing intervention protocols that (i) reproduce the non-identifiability predicted when context is latent and no context-level interventions are available, (ii) exhibit structure-mechanism confounding under latent edges when only node interventions are observed, and (iii) recover synaptic input-output relationships via targeted node interventions, consistent with our positive kernel identifiability result. Our work generalizes SCMs in a way that allows it to work in a world closer to the one we live in.

</details>


### [90] [Stable Multimodal Graph Unlearning via Feature-Dimension Aware Quantile Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03303)
*Jingjing Zhou,Yongshuai Yang,Qing Qing,Ziqi Xu,Xikun Zhang,Renqiang Luo,Ivan Lee,Feng Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FDQ框架，通过自适应识别高维输入投影层并应用分位数阈值，解决多模态图遗忘学习中参数过度编辑导致的效用灾难性下降问题，在保持遗忘效果的同时显著提升模型效用保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图遗忘学习策略对所有GNN层采用统一的参数选择与编辑方式，而多模态图的高维输入投影层编码了关键的跨模态知识，过度编辑这些敏感层会导致遗忘后效用灾难性下降，破坏稳定学习与有效隐私保护。

Method: 提出FDQ框架：1) 自适应识别高维输入投影层；2) 基于FDQ指导的分位数阈值对这些层采用更保守的抑制集构建策略；3) 保持底层重要性估计机制不变；4) 与基于对角线敏感性的参数重要性分析无缝集成，支持高效的节点和边遗忘。

Result: 在Ele-Fashion和Goodreads-NC数据集上的实验表明，FDQ在保持强效用保留的同时，有效抵御成员推断攻击，实现有效的遗忘效果。

Conclusion: FDQ为高维多模态图系统提供了一种原则性且稳健的隐私感知遗忘解决方案。

Abstract: Graph unlearning remains a critical technique for supporting privacy-preserving and sustainable multimodal graph learning. However, we observe that existing unlearning strategies tend to apply uniform parameter selection and editing across all graph neural network (GNN) layers, which is especially harmful for multimodal graphs where high-dimensional input projections encode dominant cross-modal knowledge. As a result, over-editing these sensitive layers often leads to catastrophic utility degradation after forgetting, undermining both stable learning and effective privacy protection. To address this gap, we propose FDQ, a Feature-Dimension Aware Quantile framework for multimodal graph unlearning. FDQ adaptively identifies high-dimensional input projection layers and applies more conservative, FDQ-guided quantile thresholds when constructing suppression sets, while keeping the underlying importance estimation mechanism unchanged. FDQ is seamlessly integrated with diagonal sensitivity-based parameter importance analysis to enable efficient node and edge unlearning under general forget requests. Through extensive experiments on Ele-Fashion and Goodreads-NC, we demonstrate that FDQ consistently achieves strong utility preservation while maintaining effective forgetting against membership inference attacks. Overall, FDQ offers a principled and robust solution for privacy-aware unlearning in high-dimensional multimodal graph systems.

</details>


### [91] [Distributed Learning with Adversarial Gradient Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03313)
*Nawapon Sangsiri,Yufei Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式学习中，客户端在梯度扰动（受距离约束的对抗性攻击）下，学习凸且L-平滑函数的最小可实现次优性差距及其查询复杂度，并给出了达到理论最优的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习中的隐私担忧常导致客户端返回故意篡改的梯度信息，理解在这种对抗性梯度扰动下学习问题的最优误差界和查询复杂度是核心动机。

Method: 建立了次优性差距的紧可行性阈值，并设计了在该阈值下具有可证明查询复杂度保证的算法。

Result: 得到了在对抗性梯度扰动下，学习凸且L-平滑函数的最小可达次优性差距的紧界，以及达到该差距所需的查询复杂度上界。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上刻画了分布式学习在梯度扰动场景下的最优性能边界，为隐私保护分布式优化的算法设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Privacy concerns in distributed learning often lead clients to return intentionally altered gradient information. We consider the problem of learning convex and $L$-smooth functions under adversarial gradient perturbation, where a client's gradient reply to a server query can deviate arbitrarily from the true gradient subject to a distance bound. Our study focuses on two fundamental questions: (i) what is the smallest achievable sub-optimality gap (i.e., excess error in optimization) under such responses, and (ii) how many queries are sufficient to guarantee a given sub-optimality gap? We establish tight feasibility thresholds on the sub-optimality gap and provide algorithms that achieve these thresholds with provable query complexity guarantees.

</details>


### [92] [LLM-ADAM: A Generalizable LLM Agent Framework for Pre-Print Anomaly Detection in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03328)
*Ahmadreza Eslaminia,Chuhan Cai,Cameron Smith,Ruo-Syuan Mei,Shichen Li,Rajiv Malhotra,Klara Nahrstedt,Chenhui Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-ADAM框架，通过将G代码异常检测分解为提取器、参考器和判断器三个角色，在FFF打印中实现87.5%的缺陷检测准确率，显著优于单LLM基线（59.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FFF打印的普及使非专业用户也能使用增材制造，但用户缺乏制造专业知识，可能导致切片配置或G代码编辑包含热学或几何上的有害设置。现有方法缺乏通用且可解释的打印前异常检测框架。

Method: 将G代码异常检测任务分解为三个LLM角色：Extractor-LLM将G代码映射为结构化工艺参数模式；Reference-LLM将打印机和材料文档转换为对齐的运行范围；Judge-LLM根据偏差表和G代码证据判断缺陷类别。打印机、材料和LLM主干均可互换。

Result: 在200个FFF G代码数据集（含2种打印机、2种材料、5个类别）上，最优配置达到87.5%准确率，对比最强单LLM基线为59.5%。结构化分解是主要改进来源，缺陷类别识别接近上限，残留错误集中在非缺陷样本的保守误报。

Conclusion: 结构化角色分解比LLM主干强度更能提升异常检测性能，LLM-ADAM框架具有通用性和可解释性，适用于不同打印机、材料和LLM的组合。

Abstract: Additive manufacturing (AM) continues to transform modern manufacturing by enabling flexible, on-demand production of complex geometries across diverse industries. Fused filament fabrication (FFF) has extended AM to laboratories, classrooms, and small production environments, but this accessibility shifts process-planning responsibility to users who may lack manufacturing expertise. A syntactically valid slicer profile can still encode thermally or geometrically harmful settings, and subtle G-code edits can alter extrusion, cooling, or adhesion before a print begins. Pre-print G-code screening catches accidental or adversarial machine-program errors before material or machine time is wasted. This paper proposes LLM-ADAM as a generalizable LLM framework for pre-print anomaly detection in AM. The framework decomposes the task into three roles: Extractor-LLM maps a G-code file to a structured process-parameter schema; Reference-LLM converts printer and material documentation into aligned operating ranges; and Judge-LLM interprets a deterministic deviation table and G-code evidence to decide whether a part is non-defective or belongs to an anomaly class. Printers, materials, and LLM backbones are interchangeable test conditions, not fixed assumptions. We evaluate the framework on an N=200 FFF G-code corpus spanning two desktop printer families, two materials, and five classes including non-defective, under-extrusion, over-extrusion, warping, and stringing. The best framework configuration reaches 87.5% accuracy, compared with 59.5% for the strongest engineered single-LLM baseline. The results show that structured decomposition, rather than backbone strength alone, is the dominant source of improvement, with defect classes identified at or near ceiling for leading configurations while residual errors concentrate on conservative false alarms for non-defective samples.

</details>


### [93] [Toward Structural Multimodal Representations: Specialization, Selection, and Sparsification via Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03348)
*Hahyeon Choi,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S3提出了一种结构化多模态学习框架，通过专家分解、任务路由和路径剪枝，在四个基准上取得更好性能，并发现中等稀疏度下性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态方法将所有信号编码为固定嵌入，缺乏结构化语义分解能力，难以适应不同任务的需求。

Method: S3包含三个步骤：Specialization（在共享潜在空间中形成概念级专家）、Selection（为任务自适应路由专家）、Sparsification（剪枝低效用路径以获得紧凑表示）。

Result: 在四个MultiBench基准上，S3提升了准确率，并呈现一致的倒U型稀疏-性能趋势，中等稀疏度下达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: 将多模态表示结构化为可选择的语义组件是对比学习或InfoMax方法的一种实用且有原则的替代方案。

Abstract: We propose S3 (Specialization, Selection, Sparsification), a framework that rethinks multimodal learning through a structural perspective. Instead of encoding all signals into a fixed embedding, S3 decomposes multimodal inputs into semantic experts and selectively routes them for each task. Specialization forms concept-level experts in a shared latent space, Selection adapts routing for task-specific needs, and Sparsification prunes low-utility paths to yield compact, information-minimal representations. Across four MultiBench benchmarks, S3 improves accuracy and shows a consistent reverse U-shaped sparsity-performance trend, with peak performance at intermediate sparsity. These results suggest that structuring multimodal representations as selectable semantic components provides a practical and principled alternative to contrastive learning or InfoMax-driven approaches.

</details>


### [94] [Fully Automatic Trace Gas Plume Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03372)
*Vít Růžička,David R. Thompson,Jay E. Fahlen,Amanda M. Lopez,Steven Lu,Chuchu Xiang,Holly Bender,Daniel Jensen,Philip G. Brodrick,Jake Lee,Brian Bue,Daniel H. Cusworth,Luis Guanter,Adam Chlus,Andrew Thorpe,Robert O. Green*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合机器学习形态分析与物理光谱拟合的全自动框架，用于在成像光谱仪数据中检测痕量气体点源羽流，无需人工参与。在EMIT数据上实现了两种模式：日常自动检测大事件和回顾性分析发现被遗漏的羽流。结果显示大部分大型羽流可自动检测且假阳性极低，至少25%的羽流可能被人工遗漏，并将检测扩展到NH3、NO2和CO（首次在EMIT中观测到CO羽流）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来成像光谱仪数据量将呈数量级增长，传统依赖人工审查的方式无法应对海量数据，迫切需要全自动的痕量气体点源检测方法。

Method: 结合机器学习形态分析（morphological analysis）与基于物理模型的光谱拟合（physics-based spectroscopic fitting），构建全自动检测框架。在EMIT成像光谱仪数据上部署两种运行模式：日常自动摘要（daily digest）对所有下行数据进行自动处理并标记最大事件，以及回顾性分析（retrospective analysis）找出被人工审查遗漏的羽流。

Result: 日常模式可自动检测大部分大型羽流且假阳性极低；回顾性分析表明至少25%的羽流可能被人工审查遗漏；成功将检测扩展至NH3、NO2和CO（首次在EMIT数据中观测到CO羽流）。

Conclusion: 全自动框架可有效替代人工审查处理海量光谱成像数据，在保证低假阳性的同时显著提升检测召回率，并为多种痕量气体（包括此前未被充分研究的NH3、NO2和CO）提供自动化检测能力。

Abstract: Future imaging spectrometers will increase data volumes by orders of magnitude, requiring automated detection of trace gas point sources. We present a fully automated framework that combines machine learning-based morphological analysis with physics-based spectroscopic fitting to detect plumes without human participation. Applied to EMIT imaging spectrometer data, the system operates in two modes: "daily digest" that runs automatically on all downlinked data, flagging the largest events for immediate response, and a retrospective analysis that identifies plumes missed by prior human review. The daily digest demonstrates that a significant fraction of the largest plumes can be detected automatically with negligible false positives, while retrospective analysis suggests at least 25% of plumes may have been overlooked. In addition to the previously observed methane point sources, we extend detection to three understudied trace gases: NH3, NO2 and the first observations of carbon monoxide (CO) plume in EMIT imagery.

</details>


### [95] [Learning Dynamics of Zeroth-Order Optimization: A Kernel Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03373)
*Zhe Li,Bicheng Ying,Zidong Liu,Haibo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过神经正切核（NTK）视角，解释了零阶优化方法在LLM微调中尽管理论上存在维度依赖的收敛速度劣势，却能成功应用于数十亿参数模型的反常现象。研究发现零阶SGD的学习动态由经验神经正切核（eNTK）控制，该核矩阵的保真度主要取决于扰动次数而非模型参数维度，从而证明了零阶方法在LLM微调中的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典优化理论表明零阶算法收敛速度随模型维度增加而下降，这与近期零阶方法成功应用于数十亿参数LLM微调的实验现象相矛盾，本文旨在从理论上解释这一悖论。

Method: 推导零阶SGD的单步学习动态，揭示经验神经正切核（eNTK）作为核心项；通过Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理分析发现，零阶eNTK的每个元素对应神经切向量投影到随机低维子空间的内积，其近似误差取决于模型输出大小而非参数维度。

Result: 零阶eNTK的保真度主要由扰动次数控制，具有维度无关（dimension-free）性质，这为零阶方法在LLM微调中的可扩展性提供了理论依据。

Conclusion: 基于核的框架为理解零阶方法在学习动态中的行为提供了新视角，并理论上解释了零阶方法能规避维度诅咒应用于大规模模型的原因。

Abstract: Classical optimization theory establishes that zeroth-order (ZO) algorithms suffer from a dimension-dependent slowdown, with convergence rates typically scaling with the model dimension compared to first-order methods. However, in contrast to these theoretical expectations, a growing body of recent work demonstrates the successful application of ZO methods to fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) with billions of parameters. To explain this paradox, we derive the one-step learning dynamics of ZO SGD, where the empirical Neural Tangent Kernel (eNTK) naturally emerges as the key term governing the learning behavior. Inspection of the eNTK produced by ZO SGD reveals that each element corresponds to the inner product of neural tangent vectors projected onto a random low-dimensional subspace. Thus, by invoking the Johnson-Lindenstrauss Lemma, our analysis shows that the fidelity of the ZO eNTK is governed primarily by the number of perturbations. Crucially, the approximation error depends on the model output size rather than the massive parameter dimension. This dimension-free property provides a theoretical justification for the scalability of ZO methods to LLMs finetuning tasks. We believe that this kernel-based framework offers a novel perspective for understanding ZO methods within the context of learning dynamics.

</details>


### [96] [Local Truncation Error-Guided Neural ODEs for Large Scale Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03386)
*Xiao Zhang,Yafei Li,Ruixiang Wang,Wei Wei,Shuo He,Mingliang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对物理系统中的时空预测面临的连续宏观节律与离散微观冲击的“连续-冲击”矛盾，本文提出LTE-ODE方法，将局部截断误差（LTE）重新用作无监督前向归纳偏置，通过动态空间注意力掩码在稳定区域保持高精度连续ODE演化，在冲击点自适应触发离散补偿分支，在不施加流形惩罚的情况下实现端到端训练，在多个大规模基准上取得SOTA性能，并表现出对高度非线性波动的鲁棒性及部署灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）虽擅长捕捉平滑演化，但其固有的Lipschitz连续性约束在面对突发异常时会导致严重的过平滑问题。近期基于物理信息的方法通过惩罚数值积分误差来强制流形平滑，但数学分析表明这种刚性正则化会引发梯度冲突和“注意力崩溃”（attention collapse），使模型丧失对异常的敏感性，因此需要一种能同时处理连续动态和离散冲击的方法。

Method: 提出局部截断误差引导的神经常微分方程（LTE-ODE）。核心创新在于将局部截断误差（LTE）从需消除的数值误差重新定义为无监督前向归纳偏置：将LTE映射为动态空间注意力掩码，在稳定区域保持高精度连续ODE演化的同时，在冲击点处自适应触发离散补偿分支。模型完全端到端训练，不施加任何流形惩罚正则化项。

Result: 在多个大规模基准数据集上取得最先进性能，展现出对高度非线性波动的卓越鲁棒性。积分步长消融实验表明模型具有高部署灵活性，能够自适应适应实际应用中不同的硬件内存约束。

Conclusion: LTE-ODE通过将数值误差转化为有用信号而非消除对象，成功解决了连续动态与离散冲击之间的根本矛盾，在保持高精度连续建模能力的同时，通过自适应注意力机制有效捕获异常事件，是一种高效、鲁棒且灵活的时空预测框架。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal forecasting in physical systems, such as large-scale traffic networks, requires modeling a dual dynamic: continuous macroscopic rhythms and discrete, unpredictable microscopic shocks. While Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) excel at capturing smooth evolution, their inherent Lipschitz continuity constraints inevitably cause severe over-smoothing when confronting abrupt anomalies. Recent physics-informed methods attempt to bypass this by penalizing numerical integration errors to enforce manifold smoothness. However, we mathematically reveal that such rigid regularization inherently triggers gradient conflicts and ``attention collapse,'' stripping the model of its sensitivity to anomalies. To resolve this continuity-shock dilemma, we propose Local Truncation Error-Guided Neural ODEs (LTE-ODE). Rather than treating numerical error as a nuisance to be eliminated, we innovatively repurpose the Local Truncation Error (LTE) as an unsupervised forward inductive bias. By mapping the LTE into a dynamic spatial attention mask, our architecture gracefully preserves high-precision continuous ODE evolution in stable regions, while adaptively triggering a discrete compensation branch exclusively at shock points. Trained purely end-to-end without manifold penalties, LTE-ODE achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple large-scale benchmarks, exhibiting exceptional robustness against highly non-linear fluctuations. Furthermore, our ablation on integration steps demonstrates high deployment flexibility, allowing the model to seamlessly adapt to varying hardware memory constraints in real-world applications.

</details>


### [97] [Graph Reconstruction from Differentially Private GNN Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03388)
*Rishi Raj Sahoo,Jyotirmaya Shivottam,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示差分隐私（DP）不足以保护GNN解释中的图结构隐私，提出PRIVX攻击方法，将高斯DP机制重述为DDPM前向步骤，利用逆向扩散实现高精度图结构重建。同时提供不同同质性图下的解释器选择指南，并引入PRIVF诊断工具分解泄露来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDPR等监管框架要求ML预测附带事后解释，但即使发布DP扰动的解释仍存在隐私泄露风险。现有DP标准保障被证明不充分，需重新审视图结构隐私在解释发布中的安全性。

Method: 将高斯DP机制建模为DDPM前向步骤（已知噪声水平σ(ε)），重构问题转化为以 corrupted 信号为条件的逆向扩散——即已知DP corruption下的贝叶斯去噪器。形式化分层攻击者模型 (M, ε̂, δ̂, S, ρ)，在 oblivious 和 oracle 攻击者之间插值，推导端点匹配的双边AUC界。提出PRIVF辅助诊断工具，共享扩散骨干以分解泄露来源。

Result: 在7个基准、3种DP机制、3种GNN骨干上，PRIVX在ε=5时在5/7数据集上AUC超过0.7，且在常用隐私预算内成功攻击。同质图上邻域聚合解释器（GraphLIME, GNNExplainer）比逐节点梯度解释器泄露更多结构；强异质图上顺序反转。

Conclusion: DP不足以保护GNN解释中的图结构隐私；需根据图的同质性选择解释器以缓解泄露；PRIVF可诊断泄露来源，为实践者提供分层指导。

Abstract: Regulatory frameworks such as GDPR increasingly require that ML predictions be accompanied by post-hoc explanations, even when raw data and trained models cannot be released. Differential privacy (DP) is the standard mitigation for the residual privacy risk of releasing these explanations. We show that DP is not sufficient: an adversary observing only DP-perturbed GNN explanations can reconstruct hidden graph structure with high accuracy. Our attack, PRIVX, exploits the fact that the Gaussian DP mechanism is a single DDPM forward step at known noise level σ(ε), recasting reconstruction as reverse diffusion conditioned on the corrupted signal, a principled Bayesian denoiser under known DP corruption. We formalise a stratified adversary model parameterised by (M, \hatε, \hatδ, S, ρ) that interpolates between oblivious and oracle attackers, and derive endpoint-matched two-sided bounds on reconstruction AUC. For practitioners, we provide regime-stratified guidance on explainer choice: on homophilic graphs, neighbourhood-aggregating explainers (GraphLIME, GNNExplainer) leak more structure than per-node gradient explainers under the same DP budget; on strongly heterophilic graphs the ordering reverses. We introduce PRIVF as an auxiliary diagnostic sharing the same diffusion backbone to decompose leakage into explainer-induced and intrinsic graph-distribution components. Experiments across seven benchmarks, three DP mechanisms, and three GNN backbones show PRIVX achieves AUC above 0.7 at ε = 5 on five of seven datasets, with the attack succeeding well within typically deployed privacy budgets.

</details>


### [98] [PODiff: Latent Diffusion in Proper Orthogonal Decomposition Space for Scientific Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03399)
*Onkar Jadhav,Tim French,Matthew Rayson,Nicole L. Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PODiff框架，在方差排序的POD系数空间中进行扩散，实现高维空间场的高效概率超分辨率重建，计算成本大幅降低且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维空间场的概率超分辨率在像素空间中计算成本高昂，现有方法难以兼顾效率与不确定性量化质量。

Method: 利用POD模态的正交性构建方差排序的潜在空间，在该低维系数空间执行条件扩散生成，实现结构化条件生成框架。

Result: 在西澳大利亚海表温度和受控对流扩散基准上，PODiff在重建精度上与像素空间扩散相当，但内存需求显著降低，且不确定性估计比确定性和MC Dropout基线更可靠。

Conclusion: PODiff以较低计算成本实现了可解释、校准良好的不确定性估计，为高维空间场概率超分辨率提供了一种高效且实用的方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic super-resolution of high-dimensional spatial fields using diffusion models is often computationally prohibitive due to the cost of operating directly in pixel space. We propose PODiff, a structured conditional generative framework that performs diffusion in a fixed, variance-ordered Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) coefficient space, exploiting the orthogonality of POD modes to impose an interpretable, variance-ordered latent geometry. This design enables efficient ensemble generation, preserves dominant spatial structure, and yields spatially interpretable, well-calibrated uncertainty at substantially lower computational cost. We evaluate PODiff on sea surface temperature downscaling over the West Australian coast and on a controlled advection-diffusion benchmark. PODiff achieves reconstruction accuracy comparable to pixel-space diffusion while requiring significantly less memory and producing more reliable uncertainty estimates than deterministic and Monte Carlo Dropout baselines.

</details>


### [99] [Discovering Reinforcement Learning Interfaces with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03408)
*Akshat Singh Jaswal,Ashish Baghel,Paras Chopra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LIMEN框架，利用大语言模型引导的进化算法，从原始模拟器状态中自动发现强化学习任务接口（包括观测映射和奖励函数），通过策略训练反馈迭代优化，实验表明联合进化观测与奖励比单独优化任一组件更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动构建强化学习系统的环境接口（观测和奖励函数）需要大量人工努力，现有自动化方法仅关注奖励设计而假设观测固定，未解决完整任务接口的合成问题。

Method: 提出LIMEN框架，将候选接口表示为可执行程序，利用大语言模型引导的进化算法，通过策略训练反馈进行迭代优化，联合进化观测映射和奖励函数。

Result: 在离散网格世界任务和连续控制领域（包括 locomotion 和 manipulation）中，联合进化观测和奖励发现有效接口，而单独优化任一组件在至少一个领域上失败。

Conclusion: 从原始状态自动构建RL接口可大幅减少人工工程投入，且观测和奖励组件需要协同设计，因为单组件优化会在某些领域出现灾难性失败。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning systems rely on environment interfaces that specify observations and reward functions, yet constructing these interfaces for new tasks often requires substantial manual effort. While recent work has automated reward design using large language models (LLMs), these approaches assume fixed observations and do not address the broader challenge of synthesizing complete task interfaces. We study RL task interface discovery from raw simulator state, where both observation mappings and reward functions must be generated. We propose LIMEN (Code available at https://github.com/Lossfunk/LIMEN), a LLM guided evolutionary framework that produces candidate interfaces as executable programs and iteratively refines them using policy training feedback. Across novel discrete gridworld tasks and continuous control domains spanning locomotion and manipulation, joint evolution of observations and rewards discovers effective interfaces given only a trajectory-level success metric, while optimizing either component alone fails on at least one domain. These results demonstrate that automatic construction of RL interfaces from raw state can substantially reduce manual engineering and that observation and reward components often benefit from co-design, as single-component optimization fails catastrophically on at least one domain in our evaluation suite.

</details>


### [100] [Learning to Theorize the World from Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03413)
*Doojin Baek,Gyubin Lee,Junyeob Baek,Hosung Lee,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Learning-to-Theorize学习范式及其实例化模型NEO，通过从原始非文本观测中归纳显式解释性理论（以可执行组合程序表示），实现类似人类认知的理论驱动理解与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型通常将"理解"操作化为对未来预测的准确性，但发展认知科学表明人类理解源于构建关于世界运行方式的内部理论。作者受此启发，希望让AI模型也能从原始观测中归纳出显式的解释性理论，而非仅做隐式预测。

Method: 提出Learning-to-Theorize范式，实例化为Neural Theorizer (NEO)模型。NEO是概率神经模型，包含：(1) 学习得到的"思维语言"（Language of Thought）中的潜在程序；(2) 共享的转移模型来执行这些程序。理论被表示为可执行、可组合的程序，其学到的原语可系统重组以解释新现象。

Result: 实验表明，该公式能够实现"解释驱动的泛化"，即观测可以通过生成它们的程序来被理解，而非仅通过预测未来状态。

Conclusion: NEO展示了从原始观测中学习显式理论的可能性，提供了一种不同于传统预测式世界模型的替代方案，更接近人类认知中基于理论构建的理解方式。

Abstract: What does it mean to understand the world? Contemporary world models often operationalize understanding as accurate future prediction in latent or observation space. Developmental cognitive science, however, suggests a different view: human understanding emerges through the construction of internal theories of how the world works, even before mature language is acquired. Inspired by this theory-building view of cognition, we introduce Learning-to-Theorize, a learning paradigm for inferring explicit explanatory theories of the world from raw, non-textual observations. We instantiate this paradigm with the Neural Theorizer (NEO), a probabilistic neural model that induces latent programs as a learned Language of Thought and executes them through a shared transition model. In NEO, a theory is represented as an executable, compositional program whose learned primitives can be systematically recombined to explain novel phenomena. Experiments show that this formulation enables explanation-driven generalization, allowing observations to be understood in terms of the programs that generate them.

</details>


### [101] [FIBER: A Differentially Private Optimizer with Filter-Aware Innovation Bias Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03425)
*Duc Dm,Thao Do,Minh Son Hoang,Anh Le Duc Tran,Daeyoung Kim,Huy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对差分隐私训练中滤波梯度与自适应优化器（如AdamW）不兼容的问题，提出FiBeR优化器，通过创新空间滤波、解耦观测几何与创新增益、以及滤波感知的二阶矩校准，显著提升DP训练性能并超越多个任务的最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP方法对私有化梯度进行时间滤波以降低方差，但滤波改变了DP噪声统计特性，导致为未滤波DP噪声设计的偏差校正（如减去sigma_w^2）在校准上失效，影响AdamW等自适应优化器的性能。

Method: (i) 在创新空间中进行去噪，通过滤波残差流并积分形成滤波梯度估计；(ii) 解耦两点观测几何与创新增益，实现独立调参；(iii) 引入滤波感知的二阶矩校准，以闭式推导衰减的DP噪声贡献A(omega) sigma_w^2进行修正，该闭式解适用于创新滤波器及一般稳定线性滤波器。

Result: 在视觉和语言基准上，FiBeR一致性地大幅提升DP优化器性能，在多个任务同等隐私约束下超越当前最优结果。

Conclusion: FiBeR有效解决了滤波梯度与自适应优化器之间的不兼容问题，为DP训练提供了更强的优化方案。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) training protects individual examples by adding noise to gradients, but the injected noise interacts nontrivially with adaptive optimizers. Recent DP methods temporally filter privatized gradients to reduce variance; however, filtering also changes the DP noise statistics seen by AdamW's second-moment accumulator. As a result, bias corrections derived for unfiltered DP noise, such as subtracting sigma_w squared, can become miscalibrated when filtering is present.
  We propose FiBeR, a DP optimizer designed for temporally filtered privatized gradients. FiBeR (i) performs denoising in innovation space by filtering the residual stream and integrating it to form the filtered gradient estimate, (ii) decouples the two-point observation geometry from the innovation gain to enable independent tuning, and (iii) introduces a filter-aware second-moment calibration that subtracts the attenuated DP noise contribution A(omega) sigma_w squared, where A(omega) is derived in closed form for the innovation filter and can be computed for general stable linear filters.
  Across vision and language benchmarks, FiBeR consistently demonstrates substantial improvements in the performance of DP optimizers, surpassing state-of-the-art results under equivalent privacy constraints on multiple tasks.

</details>


### [102] [DynaTab: Dynamic Feature Ordering as Neural Rewiring for High-Dimensional Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03430)
*Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib,Gianfranco Doretto,Donald A. Adjeroh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynaTab提出了一种动态特征排序方法，通过神经重连算法为高维表格数据自动调整特征顺序，显著提升了深度学习模型在表格数据上的性能，在36个真实数据集上超越了45个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维表格数据缺乏自然的特征顺序，这限制了置换敏感型深度学习模型的应用效果。

Method: 提出轻量级准则预测特征置换是否有益；通过神经重连算法动态重排特征顺序；使用位置嵌入、重要性门控和掩码注意力层的组合处理动态顺序；设计了动态特征排序（DFO）损失和分散损失进行端到端训练。

Result: 在36个真实表格数据集上，对比45个SOTA基线模型，DynaTab在统计上取得了显著增益，尤其在高维数据集上表现突出。

Conclusion: DynaTab为高维表格深度学习提供了新的范式，证明了动态特征排序的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: High-dimensional tabular data lacks a natural feature order, limiting the applicability of permutation-sensitive deep learning models. We propose DynaTab, a dynamic feature ordering-enabled architecture inspired by neural rewiring. We introduce a lightweight criterion that predicts when feature permutation will benefit a dataset by quantifying its intrinsic complexity. DynaTab dynamically reorders features via a neural rewiring algorithm and processes them through a compact, dynamic order-aware combination of separate learned positional embedding, importance-based gating, and masked attention layers, compatible with any sequence-sensitive backbone. Trained end-to-end with bespoke dynamic feature ordering (DFO) and dispersion losses, DynaTab achieves statistically significant gains, particularly on high-dimensional datasets, where it is benchmarked against 45 state-of-the-art baselines across 36 different real-world tabular datasets. Our results position DynaTab as a compelling new paradigm for high-dimensional tabular deep learning.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning via Variational Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03434)
*Yu-Ting Lee,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Fu-Chieh Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于option-critic架构的混合分层强化学习智能体，用量子变分电路替代经典组件，在标准基准环境中，使用量子特征提取器的混合智能体在节省66%可训练参数的同时超越了经典基线，但发现量子选项价值估计会造成性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究参数化量子计算在分层决策（层次强化学习）中的优势是否能够延续，弥补该领域的关键研究空白。

Method: 基于option-critic架构构建混合分层智能体，用量子变分电路替换特征提取器、选项价值函数、终止函数和内部选项策略等经典组件。

Result: 混合智能体（使用量子特征提取器）在标准基准环境中超越经典基线，节省高达66%的可训练参数；但量子选项价值估计会严重降低性能。

Conclusion: 建立了参数高效的混合分层智能体的设计原则，揭示了量子电路架构选择对性能的影响。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is one of the most challenging learning paradigms where efficacy and efficiency gains are extremely valuable. Hierarchical reinforcement learning is a variant that leverages temporal abstraction to structure decision-making. While parametrized quantum computations have shown success in non-hierarchical reinforcement learning, whether these advantages adapt to hierarchical decision-making remains a critical open question. In this work, we develop a hybrid hierarchical agent based on the option-critic architecture. This hybrid agent substitutes classical components with variational quantum circuits for feature extractors, option-value functions, termination functions, and intra-option policies. Evaluated on standard benchmarking environments, results show that a hybrid agent utilizing a quantum feature extractor can outperform classical baselines while saving up to 66\% trainable parameters. We also identify an architectural bottleneck that quantum option-value estimation severely degrades performance. Further ablation studies reveal how architectural choices of the quantum circuits affect performance. Our work establishes design principles for parameter-efficient hybrid hierarchical agents.

</details>


### [104] [Learning Generalizable Action Representations via Pre-training AEMG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03462)
*Zhenghao Huang,Huilin Yao,Kaikai Wang,Lin Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AEMG，首个大规模自监督EMG表示学习框架，通过神经肌肉收缩分词器将肌电信号转化为跨设备生理语言，在零样本和少样本场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EMG方法受限于数据异质性、标签稀缺以及缺乏统一表示框架，导致跨被试、跨设备和跨任务的泛化能力严重不足。

Method: 提出AEMG框架，包含神经肌肉收缩分词器（NCT），将EMG信号语言化为结构词和时序句；构建最大规模跨设备EMG信号词汇表，实现任意通道拓扑和采样率下的无缝迁移。

Result: 零样本LOSO准确率提升5.79-9.25%；仅用5%目标用户数据即可实现90%以上的少样本适应性能。

Conclusion: 首次提出EMG信号作为一种跨设备生理语言的概念，从海量数据中学习其语法，为单次训练、通用可用的EMG基础模型奠定基础。

Abstract: A fundamental role in decoding human motor intent and enabling intuitive human-computer interaction is played by electromyography (EMG). However, its generalization capability across subjects, devices, and tasks remains substantially limited by data heterogeneity, label scarcity, and the lack of a unified representational framework. To bridge this gap, we propose Any Electromyography (AEMG), the first large-scale, self-supervised representation learning framework for EMG. AEMG reconceptualizes neuromuscular dynamics linguistically, utilizing a novel Neuromuscular Contraction Tokenizer (NCT) to translate discrete muscle contractions into structural words and temporal activation patterns into coherent sentences. Furthermore, we compile the largest cross-device EMG signal vocabulary to date, enabling seamless transfer across arbitrary channel topologies and sampling rates. Experiments demonstrate that AEMG improves the zero-shot leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) accuracy by 5.79-9.25% compared to six state-of-the-art baselines, and achieves more than 90% few-shot adaptation performance with only 5% of target user data. Our work has proposed the concept of EMG signals as a cross-device physiological language, learned their grammar from massive amounts of data, and laid the groundwork for a single-training, universally applicable EMG foundation model.

</details>


### [105] [Bandits on graphs and structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03493)
*Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究图结构和大型结构化问题下的序列决策（bandit）问题，涵盖图bandit（谱bandit、边观察、影响力最大化）和大型行动空间（核bandit、多拟阵bandit、函数优化bandit、无穷臂bandit），旨在推动这些方法走向实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将序列决策问题的结构性解推向实际应用，重点关注图结构和大型/指数级行动空间下的挑战。

Method: 第一部分研究图bandit：奖励平滑性（谱bandit）、边观察、影响力最大化；第二部分研究大型结构化行动空间：核bandit、多拟阵bandit、函数优化bandit（含未知平滑性）、无穷多臂bandit。

Result: 系统性地综述了作者在图bandit和结构化bandit领域的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过揭示图结构和大型行动空间下的序列决策问题的结构性质，为实际应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The goal of this thesis is to investigate the structural properties of certain sequential problems in order to bring the solutions closer to a practical use. In the first part, we put a special emphasis on structures that can be represented as graphs on actions. In the second part, we study the large action spaces that can be of exponential size in the number of base actions or even infinite. For graph bandits, we consider the settings of smoothness of rewards (spectral bandits), side observations, and influence maximization. For large structured domains, we cover kernel bandits, polymatroid bandits, bandits for function optimization (including unknown smoothness), and infinitely many-arms bandits. The thesis aspires to be a survey of the author's contributions on graph and structured bandits.

</details>


### [106] [Adaptive graph-based algorithms for conditional anomaly detection and semi-supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03495)
*Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于图的半监督学习方法，包括快速近似在线标签传播算法和条件异常检测方法，并应用于医院临床异常行为识别，通过15位专家进行了人工评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决图基半监督学习在数据量大或流式数据场景下的计算和存储瓶颈，以及条件异常检测中存在的边缘点和孤立点问题。

Method: 1) 通过将邻近点坍缩为局部代表点以最小化失真，构建近似图并求解调和解；2) 正则化调和解以提升稳定性；3) 基于图连通性分析和软调和解，提出非参数条件异常检测方法。

Result: 理论和实验均表明近似图方法具有良好的表现；15位重症监护专家对条件异常检测方法进行了广泛的人工评估。

Conclusion: 所提出的快速近似在线算法和条件异常检测方法在效率和稳定性上有效，且临床异常检测有助于识别可能的医疗错误。

Abstract: We develop graph-based methods for semi-supervised learning based on label propagation on a data similarity graph. When data is abundant or arrive in a stream, the problems of computation and data storage arise for any graph-based method. We propose a fast approximate online algorithm that solves for the harmonic solution on an approximate graph. We show, both empirically and theoretically, that good behavior can be achieved by collapsing nearby points into a set of local representative points that minimize distortion. Moreover, we regularize the harmonic solution to achieve better stability properties. We also present graph-based methods for detecting conditional anomalies and apply them to the identification of unusual clinical actions in hospitals. Our hypothesis is that patient-management actions that are unusual with respect to the past patients may be due to errors and that it is worthwhile to raise an alert if such a condition is encountered. Conditional anomaly detection extends standard unconditional anomaly framework but also faces new problems known as fringe and isolated points. We devise novel nonparametric graph-based methods to tackle these problems. Our methods rely on graph connectivity analysis and soft harmonic solution. Finally, we conduct an extensive human evaluation study of our conditional anomaly methods by 15 experts in critical care.

</details>


### [107] [Bandits attack function optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03496)
*Philippe Preux,Rémi Munos,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为同时乐观优化(SOO)的确定性算法，将函数优化转化为预算约束下的序列决策问题，通过领域划分平衡探索与利用，并在CEC'2014测试套件上进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在函数评估次数受限的优化问题中，需要有效平衡全局探索（初始准均匀搜索）与局部利用（围绕潜在全局最优点的局部优化），传统方法难以在保证解的质量的同时保持计算效率。

Method: 受连续型多臂赌博机问题启发，提出同时乐观优化(SOO)算法。该算法采用确定性领域划分策略，在预算约束下动态平衡探索与利用的权衡。

Result: 在CEC'2014单目标实数参数数值优化竞赛测试套件上进行了实证评估，验证了SOO算法的数值效率和解决方案质量保证。

Conclusion: SOO算法通过领域划分的确定性方法，在预算约束下提供了有保证的解决方案质量，且具有良好的数值计算效率，是一种有效的机器学习优化方法。

Abstract: We consider function optimization as a sequential decision making problem under budget constraint. This constraint limits the number of objective function evaluations allowed during the optimization. We consider an algorithm inspired by a continuous version of a multi-armed bandit problem which attacks this optimization problem by solving the tradeoff between exploration (initial quasi-uniform search of the domain) and exploitation (local optimization around the potentially global maxima). We introduce the so-called Simultaneous Optimistic Optimization (SOO), a deterministic algorithm that works by domain partitioning. The benefit of such approach are the guarantees on the returned solution and the numerical efficiency of the algorithm. We present this machine learning approach to optimization, and provide the empirical assessment of SOO on the CEC'2014 competition on single objective real-parameter numerical optimization test-suite.

</details>


### [108] [A Hierarchical Sampling Framework for bounding the Generalization Error of Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03499)
*Dario Filatrella,Ragnar Thobaben,Mikael Skoglund*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用Wasserstein距离为层次联邦学习（HFL）建立期望泛化界，提出多层树结构数据采样框架，推导了基于Wasserstein距离的泛化界，在损失有界情况下恢复并严格蕴含现有CMI界，结合差分隐私恢复基于隐私的泛化界，并在高斯位置模型中证明其渐近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习泛化界研究主要针对单层FL结构，缺乏对HFL中分层数据采样依赖关系的理论分析；同时，已有基于互信息的界在有界损失假设下存在松弛，需要更紧且更具一般性的泛化界框架。

Method: 采用Wasserstein距离作为敏感度度量，引入超样本构造技术，通过多层树结构建模HFL中分层数据采样过程，在损失函数Lipschitz假设下推导泛化误差上界；利用FL结构特性恢复CMI界，并拓展至差分隐私场景；通过高斯位置模型分析界紧致性。

Result: 推导出基于Wasserstein距离的HFL泛化界，该界在损失有界时严格蕴含现有CMI界；结合差分隐私可恢复基于隐私的泛化界；在高斯位置模型中，所提界达到泛化误差的实际渐近速率。

Conclusion: Wasserstein距离能够有效刻画HFL中层次化数据依赖对算法泛化性能的影响，所提框架统一并改进了现有FL泛化界理论，且在实际模型中有紧致表现。

Abstract: We study expected generalization bounds for the Hierarchical Federated Learning (HFL) setup using Wasserstein distance. We introduce a generalized framework in which data is sampled hierarchically, and we model it with a multi-layered tree structure that induces dependencies among the clients' datasets. We derive generalization bounds in terms of Wasserstein distance under the Lipschitz assumption on the loss function, by applying a supersample construction that allows us to measure the sensitivity of the algorithm to the change of a single node in the sampling tree. By leveraging the FL structure, we recover and strictly imply existing state-of-the-art conditional mutual information (CMI) bounds in the case of bounded losses. We also show that our bound can be applied together with Differential Privacy assumptions, to recover generalization bounds based on algorithmic privacy. To assess the tightness of our bounds, we study the Gaussian Location Model (GLM) and show that we recover the actual asymptotic rate of the generalization error.

</details>


### [109] [Meta-Inverse Physics-Informed Neural Networks for High-Dimensional Ordinary Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03511)
*Zhao Wei,Kenneth Hor Cheng Koh,Sheng Yuan Chin,James Chun Yip Chan,Chin Chun Ooi,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MI-PINN，一种两阶段元学习框架，用于求解高维耦合ODE系统的逆问题。该方法先学习多任务共享的物理感知表征，再固定该表征优化任务特定未知量，显著降低参数搜索维度。引入自适应聚类多分支学习处理多尺度动力学。在33个耦合ODE的PBPK模型上验证，能准确恢复掩蔽动力学参数并重建缺失机制项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINN在部分可观测条件下求解高维ODE系统逆问题时，依赖任务特定的联合优化，存在优化困难且泛化能力差的问题。

Method: 将逆问题建模为两阶段元学习：先跨任务学习物理感知表征，再固定表征优化任务特定未知量。引入自适应聚类多分支学习处理多尺度动力学。

Result: 在含33个耦合ODE的全身体PBPK模型上，使用扑热息痛和茶碱的静脉/口服给药数据，MI-PINN能在有限临床观测下准确恢复掩蔽动力学参数并重建缺失机制项。

Conclusion: MI-PINN通过两阶段元学习框架有效解决了高维ODE系统逆问题中的优化困难和泛化问题，具有较高的样本效率和推理精度。

Abstract: Solving inverse problems in dynamical systems governed by high-dimensional coupled ordinary differential equations (ODEs) is a ubiquitous challenge in scientific machine learning. In many real-world applications, researchers seek to uncover unknown parameters or model unknown dynamics even as the underlying physics is only partially characterized, and observations are sparse and limited to specific measurable channels.
