While physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) are ideal for inverse inference under partial observability, existing PINNs typically rely on task-specific joint optimization, which suffers from optimization difficulties and poor generalization. In this paper, we propose a meta-inverse physics-informed neural network (MI-PINN) that reformulates inverse modeling as a two-stage meta-learning problem. MI-PINN first learns a physics-aware representation across multiple tasks, and then performs inverse modeling by optimizing task-specific unknowns while keeping the learned representation fixed. This two-stage formulation significantly reduces the parameter search dimension, thereby improving sample efficiency and enabling accurate inference. To handle multi-scale dynamics common in these high-dimensional ODE systems, we further introduce an adaptive clustering-based multi-branch learning scheme. We demonstrate the effectiveness of MI-PINN on whole-body physiologically based pharmacokinetic (PBPK) models with up to 33 coupled ODEs, using paracetamol and theophylline under intravenous and oral dosing scenarios. Experimental results show that MI-PINN enables accurate recovery of masked kinetic parameters and reconstruction of missing mechanistic terms despite limited clinical observations.

</details>


### [110] [Understanding Self-Supervised Learning via Latent Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03517)
*Fabian A Mikulasch,Friedemann Zenke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜分布匹配（LDM）框架，将自监督学习统一为对齐（最大化潜变量对数概率）和均匀性（最大化潜变量熵）的平衡，并推导出适用于高维时间序列的非线性贝叶斯滤波模型，证明预测性LDM在温和条件下可识别潜变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习虽在复杂数据中擅长学习通用表征，但缺乏统一的理论框架来解释现有方法并指导新方法设计。

Method: 将SSL形式化为潜分布匹配（LDM），即在对齐（最大化潜变量在假设模型下的对数概率）与均匀性（最大化潜变量熵防止坍塌）之间平衡；基于LDM推导出结合卡尔曼预测器的非线性、无采样的贝叶斯滤波模型。

Result: LDM框架统一了ICA、对比学习、非对比学习和预测性SSL方法（包括stop-gradient方法）；证明了在非线性预测器下，预测性LDM仍能获得可识别的潜变量表示。

Conclusion: LDM阐明了现有SSL方法背后的假设，并为开发新方法提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) excels at finding general-purpose latent representations from complex data, yet lacks a unifying theoretical framework that explains the diverse existing methods and guides the design of new ones. We cast SSL as latent distribution matching (LDM): learning representations that maximize their log-probability under an assumed latent model (alignment), while maximizing latent entropy to prevent collapse (uniformity). This view unifies independent component analysis with contrastive, non-contrastive, and predictive SSL methods, including stop gradient approaches. Leveraging LDM, we derive a nonlinear, sampling-free Bayesian filtering model with a Kalman-based predictor for high-dimensional timeseries. We further prove that predictive LDM yields identifiable latent representations under mild assumptions, even with nonlinear predictors. Overall, LDM clarifies the assumptions behind established SSL methods and provides principled guidance for developing new approaches.

</details>


### [111] [PerFlow: Physics-Embedded Rectified Flow for Efficient Reconstruction and Uncertainty Quantification of Spatiotemporal Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03548)
*Hao Zhou,Rui Zhang,Han Wan,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PerFlow，一种物理嵌入的整流流方法，用于从稀疏不规则测量中高效重建时空动力学场并量化不确定性，通过解耦观测条件与物理约束、无引导条件化及硬物理投影，实现50步采样和比2000步引导扩散快320倍的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式方法在采样时通过梯度引导同时强制数据一致性和PDE约束，导致推理速度慢且不稳定，亟需更高效的替代方案。

Method: PerFlow将观测条件化与物理约束解耦：通过将观测数据注入整流流动力学实现无引导条件化，同时利用约束保持投影（如不可压缩性或守恒律）嵌入硬物理约束，并理论上保证轨迹在采样过程中始终处于物理一致流形上。

Result: 在多种PDE系统上实现竞争性的重建精度和良好的物理一致性，仅需50步采样，推理速度比2000步的引导扩散基线快320倍。

Conclusion: PerFlow通过解耦策略显著提升了稀疏重建的效率和稳定性，为物理信息生成模型提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Reconstructing PDE-governed fields from sparse and irregular measurements is challenging due to their ill-posed nature. Deterministic surrogates are trained on dense fields that struggle with limited measurements and uncertainty quantification. Generative models, by learning distributions over spatiotemporal fields, can better handle sparsity and uncertainty. However, existing generative approaches enforce data consistency and PDE constraints simultaneously via sampling-time gradient guidance, resulting in slow and unstable inference. To this end, we propose PerFlow, a Physics-embedded rectified Flow for efficient sparse reconstruction and uncertainty quantification of spatiotemporal dynamics. PerFlow decouples observation conditioning from physics enforcement, performing guidance-free conditioning by feeding observations into rectified-flow dynamics while embedding hard physics via a constraint-preserving projection (e.g., incompressibility or conservation). Theoretically, we establish invariance guarantees to ensure that trajectories remain on the physics-consistent manifold throughout sampling. Experiments on various PDE systems demonstrate competitive reconstruction accuracy with sound physics consistency, while enabling efficient conditional sampling (e.g., 50 steps) and up to 320 faster inference than 2000-step guided diffusion baselines.

</details>


### [112] [Disentangling Shared and Task-Specific Representations from Multi-Modal Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03570)
*He Lyu,Huolin Zeng,Junren Wang,Huazhen Yang,Linchao He,Yong Chen,Zhirui Li,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer,Huan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出正交任务分解（OrthTD）框架，通过将患者表征分解为共享和任务特定子空间并施加几何正交约束，在多模态临床数据上实现多任务学习，在12430名手术患者的四项结局预测中平均AUC 87.5%、AUPRC 37.2%，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床数据具有多模态性，需要同时评估多个相关结局。现有多任务学习方法难以平衡共享表征学习与任务特定建模：硬参数共享可能因梯度冲突导致负迁移，而灵活共享仍会纠缠共享和任务特定信号。

Method: 基于统一Transformer进行多模态融合，通过正交任务分解（OrthTD）将患者表征分解为共享子空间和任务特定子空间，施加几何正交约束以减少冗余并隔离任务特定信号。

Result: 在12430名手术患者的四项结局预测任务上，OrthTD平均AUC达87.5%、平均AUPRC达37.2%，一致优于先进的表格数据方法和多任务方法，尤其在AUPRC上取得显著提升，表明在不平衡临床数据中识别罕见事件的能力更强。

Conclusion: 在共享和任务特定表征上施加非冗余约束能够改善多模态临床数据的多结局预测性能。

Abstract: Real-world clinical data is inherently multimodal, providing complementary evidence that mirrors the practical necessity of jointly assessing multiple related outcomes. Although multi-task learning can improve efficiency by sharing information across outcomes, existing approaches often fail to balance shared representation learning with outcome-specific modeling. Hard parameter sharing can trigger negative transfer when task gradients conflict, while flexible sharing may still entangle shared and task-specific signals. To address this, we propose a multi-task framework built on a unified Transformer for multimodal fusion, augmented with Orthogonal Task Decomposition (OrthTD) to split patient representations into shared and task-specific subspaces and impose a geometric orthogonality constraint to reduce redundancy and isolate task-specific signals. We evaluated OrthTD on a real-world cohort of 12,430 surgical patients for predicting four outcomes. OrthTD achieved average AUC (area under the receiver operating characteristic curve) of 87.5% and average AUPRC (area under the precision-recall curve) of 37.2%, consistently outperformed advanced tabular and multi-task methods. Notably, OrthTD achieves substantial gains in AUPRC, indicating superior performance in identifying rare events within imbalanced clinical data. These results suggest that enforcing non-redundant shared and task-specific representations can improve multi-outcome prediction from multimodal clinical data.

</details>


### [113] [Flow Matching on Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03588)
*Francesco Ruscelli,Ferdinando Zanchetta,Rita Fioresi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在黎曼对称空间上进行流匹配训练的通用框架，通过利用对称空间的代数结构将问题线性化到等距群李代数的子空间上，从而简化了测地线的处理，并在实格拉斯曼流形上进行了应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配方法主要针对欧几里得空间或简单流形，缺乏一个通用的框架来处理黎曼对称空间这类广泛而重要的流形上的流匹配问题。

Method: 利用黎曼对称空间的代数结构，将对称空间上的流匹配问题转化为其等距群李代数子空间上的流匹配问题，从而实现问题线性化，简化测地线的计算和处理。

Result: 成功将框架应用于实格拉斯曼流形 SO(n)/SO(k)×SO(n-k) 上，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种通用的黎曼对称空间流匹配训练框架，通过代数结构线性化问题，为球面、双曲空间、格拉斯曼流形等重要流形上的生成建模提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce a general framework for training flow matching models on Riemannian symmetric spaces, a large class of manifolds that includes the sphere, hyperbolic space and Grassmannians. We exploit their algebraic structure to reformulate flow matching on symmetric spaces as flow matching on a subspace of the Lie algebra of their isometry group, thus linearizing the problem and greatly simplifying the handling of geodesics. As an application, we showcase our framework on the real Grassmannians $\operatorname{SO}(n) / \operatorname{SO}(k) \times \operatorname{SO}(n-k)$.

</details>


### [114] [A Few-Step Generative Model on Cumulative Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03623)
*Zhiqi Li,Duowen Chen,Yuchen Sun,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于累积流映射（Cumulative Flow Maps）的统一少步生成建模框架，通过累积传输和参数化将局部更新与有限时间传输连接，支持少步甚至单步生成，在图像生成、几何分布建模、联合预测和SDF生成等任务中降低推理成本且保持质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散和流模型通常需要大量迭代步骤才能完成从噪声到数据的传输，推理成本高。作者希望借鉴物理传输中的流映射技术，设计一种统一的框架，使得生成模型能够在少步甚至单步内完成全局状态转换，同时保持合成质量。

Method: 提出累积流映射（Cumulative Flow Maps）抽象，将局部瞬时更新与有限时间传输连接。基于累积传输和累积参数化构建统一框架，仅需修改时间嵌入和训练目标，无需增加模型容量，即可兼容现有扩散和流模型。

Result: 在图像生成、几何分布建模、联合预测和SDF生成等多种任务上取得了有效结果，在降低推理成本的同时保持了合成质量。

Conclusion: 累积流映射提供了一种统一且高效的少步生成建模方法，具有良好的通用性和实用性，可广泛应用于现有扩散和流模型。

Abstract: We propose a unified, few-step generative modeling framework based on \emph{cumulative flow maps} for long-range transport in probability space, inspired by flow-map techniques for physical transport and dynamics. At its core is a cumulative-flow abstraction that connects local, instantaneous updates with finite-time transport, enabling generative models to reason about global state transitions. This perspective yields a unified few-step framework built on cumulative transport and \revise{cumulative} parameterization that applies broadly to existing diffusion- and flow-based models without being tied to a specific prediction \revise{instantiation}. Our formulation supports few-step and even one-step generation while preserving synthesis quality, requiring only minimal changes to time embeddings and training objectives, and no increase in model capacity. We demonstrate its effectiveness across diverse tasks, including image generation, geometric distribution modeling, joint prediction, and SDF generation, with reduced inference cost.

</details>


### [115] [Uni-OPD: Unifying On-Policy Distillation with a Dual-Perspective Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03677)
*Wenjin Hou,Shangpin Peng,Weinong Wang,Zheng Ruan,Yue Zhang,Zhenglin Zhou,Mingqi Gao,Yifei Chen,Kaiqi Wang,Hongming Yang,Chengquan Zhang,Zhuotao Tian,Han Hu,Yi Yang,Fei Wu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Uni-OPD，一种统一的学生策略蒸馏框架，通过从学生视角引入数据平衡策略促进探索、从教师视角设计结果引导的边际校准机制保证顺序一致性，在5个领域16个基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略蒸馏（OPD）虽在实践中成功，但对其可靠改进的条件缺乏理解。本文识别出两个根本瓶颈：信息状态探索不足、学生轨迹的教师监督不可靠，并据此提出统一解决方案。

Method: 提出Uni-OPD框架，采用双视角优化策略：(1) 学生视角：两种数据平衡策略促进信息性学生生成状态的探索；(2) 教师视角：提出结果引导的边际校准机制，恢复正确与错误轨迹之间的顺序一致性，确保聚合的token级指导与结果奖励的顺序一致性。

Result: 在5个领域、16个基准上开展广泛实验，涵盖单教师/多教师蒸馏、强到弱蒸馏、跨模态蒸馏等多种设置，结果验证了Uni-OPD的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: Uni-OPD为可靠的同策略蒸馏提供了统一的解决方案和实用见解，在LLM和MLLM上均表现出色。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as an effective post-training paradigm for consolidating the capabilities of specialized expert models into a single student model. Despite its empirical success, the conditions under which OPD yields reliable improvement remain poorly understood. In this work, we identify two fundamental bottlenecks that limit effective OPD: insufficient exploration of informative states and unreliable teacher supervision for student rollouts. Building on this insight, we propose Uni-OPD, a unified OPD framework that generalizes across Large Language Models (LLMs) and Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), centered on a dual-perspective optimization strategy. Specifically, from the student's perspective, we adopt two data balancing strategies to promote exploration of informative student-generated states during training. From the teacher's perspective, we show that reliable supervision hinges on whether aggregated token-level guidance remains order-consistent with the outcome reward. To this end, we develop an outcome-guided margin calibration mechanism to restore order consistency between correct and incorrect trajectories. We conduct extensive experiments on 5 domains and 16 benchmarks covering diverse settings, including single-teacher and multi-teacher distillation across LLMs and MLLMs, strong-to-weak distillation, and cross-modal distillation. Our results verify the effectiveness and versatility of Uni-OPD and provide practical insights into reliable OPD.

</details>


### [116] [Rethinking the Rank Threshold for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03724)
*Juneyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LoRA微调在神经正切核（NTK）机制下的秩需求进行研究。现有工作给出LoRA秩需满足r(r+1)/2 > KN的充分条件以避免伪局部极小，在标准RoBERTa小样本设置下要求r≥12。本文提出三个结果将二分类场景下的秩需求降至r=1：1）用非对称LoRA流形维数替代对称Sard计数，得到更宽松的容量条件r(m+n)-r² > C*·KN（C*≈1.35），在标准设置下r=1即可满足；2）交叉熵损失下Polyak--Łojasiewicz不等式完全消除秩阈值；3）Rademacher复杂度界预测当偏置项饱和时秩一方差最优，这恰好对应二分类而非多分类。实验上，四个GLUE二分类任务、三种编码器架构及RoBERTa-large上，秩一与r=12表现相当；而在多分类MNLI上最优秩高于1，符合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA微调在NTK机制下的理论分析表明，为避免伪局部极小值需要满足r(r+1)/2 > KN的充分条件，这导致在标准RoBERTa小样本设置下要求秩至少为12。但该条件对任意输出维度K均适用，其在特定场景下的紧性以及在实际使用的交叉熵损失下的适用性尚不明确。本文旨在探究二分类场景下是否能够降低所需的LoRA秩。

Method: 1）用非对称LoRA流形维数（r(m+n)-r²）替代对称Sard计数（r(r+1)/2），推导出更宽松的容量条件，并在高斯独立同分布特征假设下计算常数C*≈1.35；2）利用交叉熵损失下的Polyak--Łojasiewicz不等式分析，证明该情形下秩阈值完全消失；3）通过Rademacher复杂度界分析秩一方差最优性的条件，指出当偏置项饱和时（二分类情形）秩一方差最优，而K>2时则不然。

Result: 理论结果表明，在NTK机制下的二分类场景中，LoRA秩可以降至r=1。具体地：采用非对称流形维数后容量条件变为r(m+n)-r² > C*·KN（C*≈1.35），标准设置下r=1即可满足；交叉熵损失下PL不等式完全消除了秩阈值；Rademacher复杂度界预测秩一方差最优仅在偏置项饱和时成立，即二分类场景。实验上，四个GLUE二分类任务、三种编码器架构及RoBERTa-large上，r=1与r=12表现相当；而在多分类MNLI上最优秩高于1。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在NTK机制下的二分类LoRA微调中，秩可以降至1，显著降低了现有理论要求的r≥12。这一结论依赖于标准NTK假设，且实验验证了理论预测：二分类任务中秩一与高秩表现竞争，而多分类任务中秩一不再最优。多分类场景的扩展留待未来工作。

Abstract: A recent landscape analysis of LoRA fine-tuning in the neural tangent kernel regime establishes a sufficient condition $r(r+1)/2 > KN$ on the LoRA rank $r$ for the absence of spurious local minima under squared-error loss, prescribing $r \geq 12$ on canonical few-shot RoBERTa setups. The condition is stated for general output dimension $K$, so its sharpness in any particular regime, and its practical implication for the cross-entropy loss actually used in fine-tuning, are open. We give three results that together reduce the prescribed rank to $r = 1$ for binary classification in this regime. First, replacing the symmetric Sard-form count with the non-symmetric LoRA manifold dimension yields a strictly weaker capacity requirement, $r(m+n) - r^2 > C^* \cdot KN$ with $C^* \approx 1.35$ under Gaussian-iid features, satisfied at $r = 1$ on canonical setups. Second, in the cross-entropy setting the Polyak--Łojasiewicz inequality removes the rank threshold entirely. Third, a Rademacher-complexity bound predicts rank-one variance optimality precisely when the bias term is saturated, which is the case for binary classification but not for $K > 2$. Empirically, across four GLUE-style binary tasks, three encoder architectures, and at scale on RoBERTa-large, rank one is competitive with the existing prescription $r = 12$; on multi-class MNLI the optimal rank shifts above one, also as predicted. The binary-regime guarantees are conditional on standard NTK assumptions; the multi-class extension is left to future work.

</details>


### [117] [GEM-FI: Gated Evidential Mixtures with Fisher Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03750)
*Marco Mustafa Mohammed,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gated Evidential Mixtures (GEM)系列模型，通过门控机制和混合证据头解决证据深度学习中的过度自信、校准差和无法表示多模态认知不确定性的问题，在图像分类和OOD检测任务上显著提升了性能和校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证据深度学习(EDL)方法存在三个关键缺陷：1) 过度自信且校准差；2) 无法有效表示多模态认知不确定性；3) 通常需要多轮集成才能捕捉不确定性，增加了计算开销。

Method: 提出GEM系列三种变体：1) GEM-CORE：学习特征级能量信号并将其映射为有界门控，在支持度低时平滑抑制证据；2) GEM-MIX：引入轻量级混合证据头，通过学习的路由权重在单次推理中捕捉多模态认知不确定性；3) GEM-FI：通过Fisher信息正则化器稳定混合分配，减少头部坍缩并产生更平滑的边界不确定性。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，GEM-FI相比DAEDL准确率从91.11提升至93.75（+2.64pp），Brier得分从14.27降至6.81（-7.46），误分类检测AUPR从99.08提升至99.94（+0.86）。在认知OOD检测中，CIFAR-10→SVHN的AUPR/AUROC达到92.59/95.09，CIFAR-10→CIFAR-100达到90.20/89.06，显著优于DAEDL的85.54/89.30和88.19/86.10。

Conclusion: GEM系列模型通过距离感知的门控机制和混合证据头，在保持单次推理效率的同时，有效解决了EDL的过度自信和校准问题，并能捕捉多模态认知不确定性，在分类和OOD检测任务上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) enables single-pass uncertainty estimation by predicting Dirichlet evidence, but it can remain overconfident and poorly calibrated, and it often fails to represent multi-modal epistemic uncertainty. We introduce Gated Evidential Mixtures (GEM), a family of models that learns an in-model energy signal and uses it to gate evidential outputs end-to-end in a distance-informed manner. GEM-CORE learns a feature-level energy and maps it to a bounded gate that smoothly suppresses evidence when support is low. To capture epistemic multi-modality without multi-pass ensembling, GEM-MIX adds a lightweight mixture of evidential heads with learned routing weights while preserving single-pass inference. Finally, GEM-FI stabilizes mixture allocations via a Fisher-informed regularizer, reducing head collapse and producing smoother boundary uncertainty. Across image classification and OOD detection benchmarks, GEM improves calibration and ID/OOD separation with single-pass inference. On CIFAR-10, GEM-FI vs. DAEDL improves accuracy from 91.11 to 93.75 (+2.64 pp), reduces Brier x100 from 14.27 to 6.81 (-7.46), and also improves misclassification-detection AUPR from 99.08 to 99.94 (+0.86). For epistemic OOD detection, GEM-FI achieves AUPR/AUROC of 92.59/95.09 on CIFAR-10 to SVHN and 90.20/89.06 on CIFAR-10 to CIFAR-100, compared with 85.54/89.30 and 88.19/86.10 for DAEDL.

</details>


### [118] [Vanishing L2 regularization for the softmax Multi Armed Bandit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03752)
*Stefana-Lucia Anita,Gabriel Turinici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了L2正则化softmax策略梯度方法在正则化参数趋近于零时的理论收敛性，并验证了其在实际基准测试中的数值优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的凸性分析方法无法处理正则化参数趋于零时的收敛性问题，需要建立新的理论框架来证明L2正则化softmax策略梯度在该情况下的收敛性。

Method: 在MAB框架下，将L2正则化项（二次项）从平均奖励中减去，提出L2正则化softmax策略梯度方法，并从理论和实验两个角度分析正则化参数趋近于零时的收敛行为。

Result: 理论上证明L2正则化softmax策略梯度在正则化参数趋于零时仍然收敛；实验上确认该参数设置使L2正则化在标准基准测试中具有数值优势。

Conclusion: L2正则化softmax策略梯度方法在正则化参数趋于零时既能保证理论收敛性，又能带来实际的数值性能提升，为后续强化学习算法（如REINFORCE）奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multi Armed Bandit (MAB) algorithms are a cornerstone of reinforcement learning and have been studied both theoretically and numerically. One of the most commonly used implementation uses a softmax mapping to prescribe the optimal policy and served as the foundation for downstream algorithms, including REINFORCE. Distinct from vanilla approaches, we consider here the L2 regularized softmax policy gradient where a quadratic term is subtracted from the mean reward. Previous studies exploiting convexity failed to identify a suitable theoretical framework to analyze its convergence when the regularization parameter vanishes. We prove here theoretical convergence results and confirm empirically that this regime makes the L2 regularization numerically advantageous on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [119] [Nora: Normalized Orthogonal Row Alignment for Scalable Matrix Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03769)
*Jinghui Yuan,Jiaxuan Zou,Shuo Wang,Yong Liu,Feiping Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Nora是一种同时满足效率（类Muon预处理）、稳定性（严格尺度不变性）和速度（O(mn)复杂度）的LLM优化器，通过行方向动量投影到权重正交补空间实现稳定，利用Transformer Hessian块对角优势实现高效预处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩阵优化器无法同时满足效率、稳定性和速度三个核心需求：Muon计算成本高昂，RMNP存在径向抖动导致稳定性不足。

Method: 1) 通过行方向动量投影到权重的正交补空间，显式稳定权重范数和角速度；2) 利用Transformer Hessian的块对角优势，近似结构化预处理，实现O(mn)最优计算复杂度。

Result: 两行代码的简洁实现，初步实验验证Nora在大规模训练中的高效性和潜力。

Conclusion: Nora是首个同时严格满足效率、稳定性、速度三个设计准则的可扩展优化器，并建立了对应的缩放定理。

Abstract: Matrix-based optimizers have demonstrated immense potential in training Large Language Models (LLMs), however, designing an ideal optimizer remains a formidable challenge. A superior optimizer must satisfy three core desiderata: efficiency, achieving Muon-like preconditioning to accelerate optimization; stability, strictly adhering to the scale-invariance inherent in neural networks; and speed, minimizing computational overhead. While existing methods address these aspects to varying degrees, they often fail to unify them, either incurring prohibitive computational costs like Muon, or allowing radial jitters that compromise stability like RMNP. To bridge this gap, we propose Nora, an optimizer that rigorously satisfies all three requirements. Nora achieves training stability by explicitly stabilizing weight norms and angular velocities through row-wise momentum projection onto the orthogonal complement of the weights. Simultaneously, by leveraging the block-diagonal dominance of the Transformer Hessian, Nora effectively approximates structured preconditioning while maintaining an optimal computational complexity of $\mathcal{O}(mn)$. Furthermore, we prove that Nora is a scalable optimizer and establish its corresponding scaling theorems. With a streamlined implementation requiring only two lines of code, our preliminary experiments validate Nora as an efficient and highly promising optimizer for large-scale training.

</details>


### [120] [Spatiotemporal Convolutions on EEG signal -- A Representation Learning Perspective on Efficient and Explainable EEG Classification with Convolutional Neural Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03874)
*Laurits Dixen,Stefan Heinrich,Paolo Burelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在脑机接口运动想象分类任务中，使用2D时空卷积替代1D分离卷积的方法。实验表明，2D卷积在高维任务中能显著减少训练时间且保持性能不变，但两者在内部表征几何结构上存在显著差异，建议在复杂多变量信号处理中采用2D卷积层以加快训练和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数基于浅层CNN的EEG信号分类模型采用沿空间和时间维度的独立1D卷积层进行拼接，且中间不含非线性激活。本文旨在探索一种使用2D时空卷积的替代编码方式，并比较其与1D卷积在性能、训练效率和内部表征上的差异。

Method: 在低维（3通道）和高维（22通道）BCI运动想象分类任务中，分别测试了1D CNN、2D CNN以及CNN+Transformer混合模型。通过对比训练时间、分类性能、谱特征重要性和表征相似性（representational similarity）来系统分析1D与2D卷积的差异。

Result: 2D卷积在高维任务中显著降低训练时间，同时保持与1D卷积相当的分类性能。谱特征重要性分析未发现两者差异，但表征相似性分析揭示1D和2D模型产生截然不同的表征几何结构（representational geometries）。

Conclusion: 建议在高维EEG信号处理中使用带有2D卷积层的改进模型，以实现更快的训练和推理。同时强调在处理复杂多变量信号时，需关注架构驱动的编码方式对内部表征的影响，而不仅仅是关注性能指标。

Abstract: Classification of EEG signals using shallow Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) is a prevalent and successful approach across a variety of fields. Most of these models use independent one-dimensional (1D) convolutional layers along the spatial and temporal dimensions, which are concatenated without a non-linear activation layer between. In this paper, we investigate an alternative encoding that operates a bi-dimensional (2D) spatiotemporal convolution. While 2D convolutions are numerically identical to two concatenated 1D convolutions along the two dimensions, the impact on learning is still uncertain. We test 1D and 2D CNNs and a CNN+transformer hybrid model in a low-dimensional (3-channel) and a high-dimensional (22-channel) BCI motor imagery classification task. We observe that 2D convolutions significantly reduce training time in high-dimensional tasks while maintaining performance. We investigate the root of this improvement and find no difference in spectral feature importance. However, a clear pattern emerges in representational similarity across models: 1D and 2D models yield vastly different representational geometries. Overall, we suggest an improved model with a 2D convolutional layer for faster training and inference. We also highlight the importance of architecturally-driven encoding when processing complex multivariate signals, as reflected in internal representations rather than purely in performance metrics.

</details>


### [121] [Graph Convolutional Support Vector Regression for Robust Spatiotemporal Forecasting of Urban Air Pollution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03795)
*Nourin Jahan,Madhurima Panja,Muhammed Navas T,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出图卷积支持向量回归（GCSVR）框架，结合图卷积学习空间依赖与支持向量回归抗异常值的时间建模，在德里和孟买37+18个监测站数据上验证了多时间尺度预测的准确性和鲁棒性，并集成分位预测实现不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空气质量预测面临非线性、非平稳、时空依赖及异常观测值（交通拥堵、工业排放、季节气象变异）等多重挑战，现有方法在鲁棒性和预测精度上存在不足。

Method: 提出GCSVR框架：图卷积学习站间空间依赖结构，支持向量回归建模非线性时间动态并降低对离群观测的敏感性；结合共形预测生成校准后的预测区间。

Result: 在德里（37站）和孟买（18站）两种不同城市环境（内陆vs沿海）的多步长预测中，GCSVR持续提升预测精度，在季节变化和异常污染事件中保持稳定性能，统计检验证实其在两城市间的可靠性。

Conclusion: GCSVR是一种鲁棒的时空空气质量预测方法，集成的共形预测提供了具有实用价值的不确定性量化，可支持空气质量监测和公共卫生决策。

Abstract: Urban air quality forecasting is challenging because pollutant concentrations are nonlinear, nonstationary, spatiotemporally dependent, and often affected by anomalous observations caused by traffic congestion, industrial emissions, and seasonal meteorological variability. This study proposes a Graph Convolutional Support Vector Regression (GCSVR) framework for robust spatiotemporal forecasting of urban air pollution. The model combines graph convolutional learning to capture inter-station spatial dependence with support vector regression to model nonlinear temporal dynamics while reducing sensitivity to outlier observations. The proposed framework is evaluated using air quality records from 37 monitoring stations in Delhi and 18 stations in Mumbai, representing inland and coastal metropolitan environments in India. Forecasting performance is assessed across multiple horizons and compared with established temporal and spatiotemporal benchmarks. The results show that GCSVR consistently improves predictive accuracy and maintains stable performance across seasons and outlier-prone pollution episodes. Statistical test further confirms the reliability of the proposed approach across the two cities. Finally, conformal prediction is integrated with GCSVR to generate calibrated prediction intervals, enhancing its practical value for uncertainty-aware air quality monitoring and public health decision-making.

</details>


### [122] [Realizable Bayes-Consistency for General Metric Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03823)
*Dan Tsir Cohen,Steve Hanneke,Aryeh Kontorovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在可实现设定下，研究了通用度量损失学习中的强贝叶斯一致性，将经典结论从0-1分类和实值回归拓展到一般度量损失。我们给出了假设类存在分布无关学习规则（其风险几乎必然收敛到类内最优风险）的充要条件，主要贡献是刻画了一种组合障碍：引入了无限非递减(γ_k)-Littlestone树的概念，将Littlestone树结构推广到度量损失场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决文献中提出的一个开放问题（可实现情形），将强通用贝叶斯一致性的刻画从0-1分类和实值回归拓展到一般度量损失场景，为理解更广泛的损失函数下的学习可行性提供理论基础。

Method: 引入了无限非递减(γ_k)-Littlestone树的概念，作为度量损失设定下组合障碍的刻画工具，并给出了假设类可学习的充要条件。

Result: 给出了在可实现设定下，对于一般度量损失，假设类存在分布无关学习规则使得风险几乎必然收敛到零（类内最优风险）的充要条件。

Conclusion: 将Littlestone树结构成功推广到度量损失设定，为通用度量损失下的学习理论提供了完整的组合刻画。

Abstract: We study strong universal Bayes-consistency in the realizable setting for learning with general metric losses, extending classical characterizations beyond $0$-$1$ classification \citep{bousquet_theory_2021, hanneke2021universalbayesconsistencymetric} and real-valued regression \citep{attias_universal_2024}. Given an instance space $(\mathcal X,ρ)$, a label space $(\mathcal Y,\ell)$ with possibly unbounded loss, and a hypothesis class $\mathcal H \subseteq \mathcal Y^{\mathcal X}$, we resolve the realizable case of an open problem presented in \citet{pmlr-v178-cohen22a}. Specifically, we find the necessary and sufficient conditions on the hypothesis class $\mathcal H$ under which there exists a distribution-free learning rule whose risk converges almost surely to the best-in-class risk (which is zero) for every realizable data-generating distribution. Our main contribution is this sharp characterization in terms of a combinatorial obstruction: Similarly to \citet{attias2024optimallearnersrealizableregression}, we introduce the notion of an infinite non-decreasing $(γ_k)$-Littlestone tree, where $γ_k \to \infty$. This extends the Littlestone tree structure used in \citet{bousquet_theory_2021} to the metric loss setting.

</details>


### [123] [A Domain Incremental Continual Learning Benchmark for ICU Time Series Model Transportability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03832)
*Ryan King,Conrad Krueger,Ethan Veselka,Tianbao Yang,Bobak J. Mortazavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出一种跨区域医院间机器学习模型迁移的基准测试方法，将模型迁移视为域增量学习问题，并评估了数据回放和弹性权重巩固两种方法在保持原始域特征的同时学习新域信息的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型医院因数据收集、标注和计算资源有限，难以独立训练临床结果预测模型。虽然可以迁移大型医院训练的模型进行微调，但这些模型通常在单一医院数据上训练，泛化能力存疑。研究发现美国不同地区的测量分布和频率存在显著差异，因此需要评估模型跨区域迁移的能力。

Method: 提出一个基准测试来评估机器学习模型从源域向美国不同区域的迁移能力。将跨区域模型迁移形式化为域增量学习问题，保持患者结果预测任务不变但输入数据分布变化。评估两种域增量学习方法：1）数据回放——存储先前数据源的样例用于当前源微调；2）弹性权重巩固（EWC）——模型参数正则化方法，维持对多个数据源都重要的特征。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体实验结果，仅描述了提出的基准测试框架和评估方法。

Conclusion: 该研究通过域增量学习框架解决临床预测模型跨区域迁移的泛化性问题，为小医院利用大医院预训练模型提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: In recent years, machine learning has made significant progress in clinical outcome prediction, demonstrating increasingly accurate results. However, the substantial resources required for hospitals to train these models, such as data collection, labeling, and computational power, limit the feasibility for smaller hospitals to develop their own models. An alternative approach involves transferring a machine learning model trained by a large hospital to smaller hospitals, allowing them to fine-tune the model on their specific patient data.
  However, these models are often trained and validated on data from a single hospital, raising concerns about their generalizability to new data. Our research shows that there are notable differences in measurement distributions and frequencies across various regions in the United States. To address this, we propose a benchmark that tests a machine learning model's ability to transfer from a source domain to different regions across the country. This benchmark assesses a model's capacity to learn meaningful information about each new domain while retaining key features from the original domain.
  Using this benchmark, we frame the transfer of a machine learning model from one region to another as a domain incremental learning problem. While the task of patient outcome prediction remains the same, the input data distribution varies, necessitating a model that can effectively manage these shifts. We evaluate two popular domain incremental learning methods: data replay, which stores examples from previous data sources for fine-tuning on the current source, and Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC), a model parameter regularization method that maintains features important for both data sources.

</details>


### [124] [Memory-Efficient Continual Learning with CLIP Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03866)
*Ryan King,Gang Li,Bobak Mortazavi,Tianbao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对CLIP模型在增量学习中因内存不足导致对比损失性能下降的问题，提出了一种内存高效、分布鲁棒的方法，通过按类别动态重新加权损失来减少灾难性遗忘，在少量内存下实现快速适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP模型在适应新数据时容易遗忘先前知识，而现有方法在内存缓冲区较小时，CLIP的对比损失会显著退化，导致旧任务性能下降。因此需要一种内存高效的方法来缓解灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出一种分布鲁棒的动态损失重加权方法，在训练过程中按类别动态调整损失权重，以在有限内存下平衡新旧任务的学习。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、ImageNet1K（类增量设置）和DomainNet（域增量设置）上进行了测试，在最小内存使用下实现了快速适应并显著减少了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 所提出的内存高效、分布鲁棒的方法能有效解决CLIP在增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，即使在内存极其有限的情况下也能保持良好性能。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models excel at understanding image-text relationships but struggle with adapting to new data without forgetting prior knowledge. To address this, models are typically fine-tuned using both new task data and a memory buffer of past tasks. However, CLIP's contrastive loss suffers when the memory buffer is small, leading to performance degradation on previous tasks. We propose a memory-efficient, distributionally robust method that dynamically reweights losses per class during training. Our approach, tested on class incremental settings (CIFAR-100, ImageNet1K) and a domain incremental setting (DomainNet) adapts CLIP models quickly while minimizing catastrophic forgetting, even with minimal memory usage.

</details>


### [125] [Flow Sampling: Learning to Sample from Unnormalized Densities via Denoising Conditional Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03984)
*Aaron Havens,Brian Karrer,Neta Shaul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Sampling，一种基于扩散模型和流匹配的无数据采样框架，用于从未归一化密度中高效采样，通过构造条件漂移项回归能量函数导出的去噪扩散漂移，并利用插值过程最小化训练时能量函数评估次数，方法可自然推广到黎曼流形，在多个任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从未归一化密度中采样时，能量函数评估通常计算成本高昂，因此需要学习一个高效的采样器。本文旨在解决这一问题，提出一种数据无关的框架，在减少能量函数调用次数的同时实现高效可扩展的采样。

Method: 基于扩散模型和流匹配，训练目标以噪声样本为条件，回归到由能量函数构造的去噪扩散漂移（而非传统扩散模型中基于数据样本回归加噪漂移）；利用插值过程（interpolant process）减少训练中能量函数评估次数；方法可自然推广到黎曼流形，并导出了常曲率流形（包括超球面和双曲空间）上条件漂移的闭式解。

Result: 在合成能量基准、小肽、大规模摊销分子构象生成以及球面分布采样等任务上均取得了优异的实证性能。

Conclusion: Flow Sampling是一种高效、可扩展且能泛化到非欧几何空间的无数据采样方法，有效解决了未归一化密度采样中能量函数评估成本高的问题。

Abstract: Sampling from unnormalized densities is analogous to the generative modeling problem, but the target distribution is defined by a known energy function instead of data samples. Because evaluating the energy function is often costly, a primary challenge is to learn an efficient sampler. We introduce Flow Sampling, a framework built on diffusion models and flow matching for the data-free setting. Our training objective is conditioned on a noise sample and regresses onto a denoising diffusion drift constructed from the energy function. In contrast, diffusion models' objective is conditioned on a data sample and regresses onto a noising diffusion drift. We utilize the interpolant process to minimize the number of energy function evaluations during training, resulting in an efficient and scalable method for sampling unnormalized densities. Furthermore, our formulation naturally extends to Riemannian manifolds, enabling diffusion-based sampling in geometries beyond Euclidean space. We derive a closed-form formula for the conditional drift on constant curvature manifolds, including hyperspheres and hyperbolic spaces. We evaluate Flow Sampling on synthetic energy benchmarks, small peptides, large-scale amortized molecular conformer generation, and distributions supported on the sphere, demonstrating strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [126] [From Data Lifting to Continuous Risk Estimation: A Process-Aware Pipeline for Predictive Monitoring of Clinical Pathways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03895)
*Pasquale Ardimento,Mario Luca Bernardi,Marta Cimitile,Samuele Latorre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复现且具有过程感知的临床路径预测监控流水线，通过数据提升、时间重建、事件日志构建、前缀表示和预测建模，在COVID-19临床路径上进行评估，以ICU入院为预测目标，逻辑回归取得了最佳性能（AUC 0.906, F1-score 0.835），并发现预测性能随临床事件积累而逐步提升（AUC从0.642提升至0.942）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回顾性流程挖掘在处理部分观测的患者轨迹时存在局限性，缺乏对进行中临床路径的连续推理能力，因此需要一种过程感知的预测监控方法来支持动态风险评估。

Method: 提出一个可复现的过程感知流水线，包括数据提升、时间重建、事件日志构建、前缀表示和预测建模；采用病例级划分（测试集896名患者），在COVID-19临床路径上以ICU入院为预测目标进行实验。

Result: 逻辑回归表现最佳（AUC 0.906, F1-score 0.835）；前缀分析显示预测性能随事件积累逐步提升，AUC从早期阶段的0.642提升至后期阶段的0.942。

Conclusion: 医疗领域的预测监控应被视为一个连续、动态感知的过程，风险估计随患者轨迹演变逐步精炼；预测信号沿临床路径渐进式涌现，过程感知表示能够实现有效的早期风险评估。

Abstract: This paper presents a reproducible and process-aware pipeline for predictive monitoring of clinical pathways. The approach integrates data lifting, temporal reconstruction, event log construction, prefix-based representations, and predictive modeling to support continuous reasoning on partially observed patient trajectories, overcoming the limitations of traditional retrospective process mining. The framework is evaluated on COVID-19 clinical pathways using ICU admission as the prediction target, considering 4,479 patient cases and 46,804 prefixes. Predictive models are trained and evaluated using a case-level split, with 896 patients in the test set. Logistic Regression achieves the best performance (AUC 0.906, F1-score 0.835). A detailed prefix-based analysis shows that predictive performance improves progressively as new clinical events become available, with AUC increasing from 0.642 at early stages to 0.942 at later stages of the pathway. The results highlight two key findings: predictive signals emerge progressively along clinical pathways, and process-aware representations enable effective early risk estimation from evolving patient trajectories. Overall, the findings suggest that predictive monitoring in healthcare is best conceived as a continuous, dynamically aware process, in which risk estimates are progressively refined as the patient journey evolves.

</details>


### [127] [Optimal Posterior Sampling for Policy Identification in Tabular Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03921)
*Cyrille Kone,Kevin Jamieson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合后验采样与在线学习的随机高效算法，用于有限时域马尔可夫决策过程中的最佳策略识别，在计算复杂度和样本复杂度上均达到渐近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有(ε, δ)-PAC策略识别方法计算成本高且对log(1/δ)依赖次优，需要更高效实用的算法。

Method: 将后验采样（posterior sampling）与在线学习算法相结合，指导MDP中的探索过程，每个episode的计算复杂度为O(S²AH)。

Result: 在样本复杂度上达到渐近最优，后验收缩率最优，计算复杂度与标准基于模型的方法相当，避免了次优的多项式log(1/δ)依赖。

Conclusion: 为表格型MDP中的高效策略识别提供了理论见解和实用工具，优于MOCA和PEDEL等现有算法。

Abstract: We study the $(\varepsilon, δ)$-PAC policy identification problem in finite-horizon episodic Markov Decision Processes. Existing approaches provide finite-time guarantees for approximate settings ($\varepsilon>0$) but suffer from high computational cost, rendering them hard to implement, and also suffer from suboptimal dependence on $\log(1/δ)$. We propose a randomized and computationally efficient algorithm for best policy identification that combines posterior sampling with an online learning algorithm to guide exploration in the MDP. Our method achieves asymptotic optimality in sample complexity, also in terms of posterior contraction rate, and runs in $O(S^2AH)$ per episode, matching standard model-based approaches. Unlike prior algorithms such as MOCA and PEDEL, our guarantees remain meaningful in the asymptotic regime and avoid sub-optimal polynomial dependence on $\log(1/δ)$. Our results provide both theoretical insights and practical tools for efficient policy identification in tabular MDPs.

</details>


### [128] [Integrating Feature Correlation in Differential Privacy with Applications in DP-ERM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03945)
*Tianyu Wang,Luhao Zhang,Rachel Cummings*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CorrDP框架，一种考虑敏感与非敏感特征相关性的差分隐私松弛定义，通过全变差距离量化相关性，在保护敏感特征的同时放松对非敏感特征的隐私约束，并设计了基于距离感知噪声的DP-ERM算法，在合成和真实数据集上优于标准差分隐私方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准差分隐私对所有特征施加统一的隐私约束，忽略了实际场景中敏感特征与不敏感特征之间的本质区别，导致隐私预算浪费在不敏感特征上，限制了模型的实用性。

Method: 提出CorrDP框架，使用全变差距离量化敏感特征与不敏感特征之间的相关性；设计基于距离感知噪声的DP-ERM算法；当相关距离未知时，从数据集中估计相关距离并提供可比的隐私-效用保证。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，CorrDP框架下的DP-ERM算法在存在不敏感特征时持续优于标准差分隐私框架。

Conclusion: CorrDP通过区分敏感与不敏感特征并建模其相关性，在保证敏感特征隐私的同时提升模型效用，为异构隐私保护提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Standard differential privacy imposes uniform privacy constraints across all features, overlooking the inherent distinction between sensitive and insensitive features in practice. In this paper, we introduce a relaxed definition of differential privacy that accounts for such heterogeneity, allowing certain features to be treated as insensitive even when correlated with sensitive ones. We propose a correlation-aware framework, $\textsf{CorrDP}$, which relaxes privacy for insensitive features while accounting for their correlations with sensitive features, with the correlations quantified using total variation distance. We design algorithms for differentially private empirical risk minimization (DP-ERM) under the $\textsf{CorrDP}$ framework, incorporating distance-dependent noise into gradients for improved theoretical utility guarantees. When the correlation distance is unknown, we estimate it from the dataset and show that it achieves a comparable privacy-utility guarantee. We perform experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets and show that $\textsf{CorrDP}$-based DP-ERM algorithms consistently outperform the standard DP framework in the presence of insensitive features.

</details>


### [129] [Pretrained Model Representations as Acquisition Signals for Active Learning of MLIPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03964)
*Eszter Varga-Umbrich,Shikha Surana,Paul Duckworth,Jules Tilly,Olivier Peltre,Zachary Weller-Davies*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用预训练MACE势能模型潜空间中的神经正切核（NTK）和激活核作为主动学习采集信号，无需额外不确定性头部或系综集成，在反应性化学基准上相比基线方法平均减少38%能量误差和28%力误差所需数据量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习可降低反应性化学MLIP训练中对昂贵量子化学标签的依赖，但采集规则的有效性至关重要。现有方法需要辅助不确定性头部、贝叶斯训练或委员会集成，增加计算开销。本文探究预训练MLIP的潜空间是否已包含足够信息以实现有效采集。

Method: 从预训练MACE势能模型中直接导出两种采集信号：有限宽神经正切核（NTK）和基于潜空间隐层特征的激活核。在反应性化学基准上，对比固定描述子基线、委员会分歧和随机采集策略，评估数据效率提升幅度。

Result: 两种核方法一致优于所有基线，减少达到性能目标所需数据量：能量误差平均降低38%，力误差平均降低28%。预训练模型诱导的相似空间保留了化学结构信息，且比随机初始化或固定描述子核提供更可靠的残差不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 预训练使潜空间几何与模型误差对齐，为反应性MLIP微调提供了实用且充分的主动学习采集信号，无需额外不确定性机制。

Abstract: Training machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for reactive chemistry is often bottlenecked by the high cost of quantum chemical labels and the scarcity of transition state configurations in candidate pools. Active learning (AL) can mitigate these costs, but its effectiveness hinges on the acquisition rule. We investigate whether the latent space of a pretrained MLIP already contains the information necessary for effective acquisition, eliminating the need for auxiliary uncertainty heads, Bayesian training and fine-tuning, or committee ensembles. We introduce two acquisition signals derived directly from a pretrained MACE potential: a finite-width neural tangent kernel (NTK) and an activation kernel built from hidden latent space features. On reactive-chemistry benchmarks, both kernels consistently outperform fixed-descriptor baselines, committee disagreement, and random acquisition, reducing the data required to reach performance targets by an average of 38% for energy error and 28% for force error. We further show that the pretrained model induces similarity spaces that preserve chemically meaningful structure and provide more reliable residual uncertainty estimates than randomly initialised or fixed-descriptor-based kernels. Our results suggest that pretraining aligns latent-space geometry with model error, yielding a practical and sufficient acquisition signal for reactive MLIP fine-tuning.

</details>


### [130] [A Closed-Form Adaptive-Landmark Kernel for Certified Point-Cloud and Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04046)
*Sushovan Majhi,Atish Mitra,Žiga Virk,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PALACE是PLACE的数据自适应版本，通过少量交叉验证调参和覆盖论核心，提供四种封闭形式的理论保证，在多个图数据集上达到或超越现有最优的持久图分类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久图分析方法（如PLACE）使用均匀网格编码拓扑特征，在处理密度不均的持久图时效率低下。本文旨在设计一种数据自适应的持久图分析框架，在保持理论可解释性的同时提升分类性能和数据效率。

Method: 提出PALACE框架，核心包括：(i) 基于Lebesgue数准则的覆盖论方法，自适应放置地标；(ii) 利用最远点采样近似最优k-中心覆盖半径；(iii) 推导出核RKHS分类率、过滤选择规则等封闭形式；(iv) 提供非渐近Pinelis和渐近高斯形式的逐预测置信度证书。

Result: PALACE在Orbit5k上达91.3±1.0%（匹配Persformer），在COX2和MUTAG上领先所有基于持久图的方法，在DHFR上接近ECP（1个百分点内）。在8倍域膨胀下自适应放置保持94%准确率，而均匀网格降至25%（4类数据随机水平）。

Conclusion: PALACE作为封闭形式的持久图分类方法，在无需梯度训练的条件下即可达到与深度方法相当的性能，特别在域膨胀场景下展现出强大的鲁棒性，验证了自适应地标策略的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce PALACE (Persistence Adaptive-Landmark Analytic Classification Engine), the data-adaptive companion to PLACE, paying a small cross-validation tier on three knobs (budget, radii, bandwidth; $\leq 5$ choices each). A cover-theoretic core (Lebesgue-number criterion on the landmark cover) yields four closed-form guarantees. (i) A structural lower distortion bound $λ(τ;ν)$ on $\mathcal{D}_n$ under cross-diagram non-interference, with a $(D/L)^2$ budget reduction over the uniform grid when diagrams concentrate. (ii) Equal weights $w_k = K^{-1/2}$ maximizing $λ$, and farthest-point-sampling positions $2$-approximating the optimal $k$-center covering radius; both derived from training labels alone, no gradient training. (iii) A kernel-RKHS classification rate $O((k-1)\sqrt{K}/(γ\sqrt{m_{\min}}))$ with binary necessity threshold $m = Ω(\sqrt K/γ)$ from a matching Le Cam lower bound, and a closed-form filtration-selection rule. The kernel-Mahalanobis margin $\hatρ_{\mathrm{Mah}}$ is the strongest closed-form ranker across the chemical-graph pool (mean Spearman $ρ\approx +0.60$); the isotropic surrogate $\hatγ/\sqrt{K}$ admits a selection-consistency rate, and $\widehatλ$ from (i) provides an independent data-level signal (positive on COX2 and PTC). (iv) A per-prediction certificate, in non-asymptotic Pinelis and asymptotic Gaussian forms, with no calibration split. Empirically, PALACE is the strongest closed-form diagram-based method on Orbit5k ($91.3 \pm 1.0\%$, matching Persformer), leads every diagram-based competitor on COX2 and MUTAG, and is competitive on DHFR (within 1 pp of ECP). At $8\times$ domain inflation, adaptive placement maintains $94\%$ while the uniform grid collapses to chance ($25\%$ on 4-class data).

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [A Predefined-Time Neurodynamic Approach with Time-Varying Coefficients for Mixed Variational Inequalities and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02893)
*Vajahat Karim Khan,Md. Kalimuddin Ahmad*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有时变系数的预定义时间神经动力学方法，用于求解混合变分不等式问题。通过一阶近端神经动力学模型，实现了用户预设时间内的收敛，并基于Lyapunov分析严格证明了预定义时间稳定性。方法对 bounded disturbances 具有鲁棒性，并在复合优化和极小极大优化问题中展示了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经动力学方法求解混合变分不等式问题时，收敛时间通常依赖于初始条件或系统参数，难以在用户预设的固定时间内保证收敛。本文旨在设计一种能够在任意初始条件下、在用户预先指定的时间内收敛的神经动力学框架。

Method: 提出了具有时变系数的一阶近端神经动力学模型，利用时变增益调节收敛速度；通过Lyapunov方法在强伪单调性和Lipschitz连续性假设下建立预定义时间稳定性，并推导收敛时间与系统参数的显式关系；同时分析了方法对 bounded disturbances 的鲁棒性。

Result: 理论分析表明所提方法能在用户预设的有限时间内收敛，且收敛时间与初始条件无关。数值仿真验证了该方法在复合优化和极小极大优化问题中的快速收敛性能和有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的预定义时间神经动力学方法有效解决了混合变分不等式问题的固定时间收敛难题，具有鲁棒性和可调性，为相关优化问题提供了一种高效的求解框架。

Abstract: This paper proposes a predefined-time (PDT) neurodynamic approach with time-varying coefficients for solving mixed variational inequality problems (MVIs). A class of first-order proximal neurodynamic models is developed to guarantee convergence within a user-prescribed time from arbitrary initial conditions.
  PDT stability is rigorously established via Lyapunov analysis under strong pseudomonotonicity and Lipschitz continuity assumptions, and explicit relationships between convergence time and system parameters are derived. The robustness of the proposed method against bounded disturbances is also analyzed. Applications to composite and minimax optimization problems, together with numerical simulations, demonstrate the effectiveness and fast convergence performance of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [132] [Preventive Maintenance of a Two-Unit Priority Standby System with Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02895)
*Alexandros Carballo,Marelys Crespo,José E Valdés*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了两单元优先备用系统在预防性维修下的最优维护策略，推导了维护改善系统平均故障时间（MTTF）的充要条件，并针对优先单元风险率呈浴盆形或倒浴盆形的情况，给出了阈值时间和最优维护时间的显式表达式，同时利用随机序理论比较了不同系统的平均寿命及维护参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可修系统的可靠性分析中，最优维护策略至关重要。现有研究缺乏对预防性维护如何影响两单元优先备用系统MTTF的充要条件分析，以及针对复杂风险率形状（如浴盆形）的优化方法。

Method: 1) 建立两单元优先备用系统模型，推导维护改善MTTF的充要条件；2) 针对优先单元风险率呈浴盆形或倒浴盆形，利用风险率和平均剩余寿命函数推导阈值时间和最优维护时间的显式表达式；3) 采用随机序技术比较不同系统的平均寿命、阈值时间和最优维护时间。

Result: 得到了维护改善MTTF的充要条件；建立了浴盆形和倒浴盆形风险率下阈值时间和最优维护时间的存在性条件及显式表达式；通过随机序比较给出了不同系统间平均寿命和维护参数的序关系。

Conclusion: 本文为两单元优先备用系统的预防性维护提供了理论依据和量化工具，所提出的充要条件和显式表达式可指导实际维护决策，随机序比较方法为系统设计选择提供了参考。

Abstract: Optimal maintenance policies play an important role in the reliability analysis of repairable systems. In this paper, we study a two-unit priority standby system with a repair facility subject to preventive maintenance. In the general case, we derive necessary and sufficient conditions under which maintenance improves the mean time to system failure. When the hazard rate of the priority unit exhibits either a bathtub-shaped or an upside-down bathtub-shaped, we establish conditions for the existence of both a threshold time beyond which maintenance enhances the mean time to system failure and an optimal maintenance time. We also derive explicit expressions for these quantities in terms of the hazard rate and the mean residual life function. Furthermore, stochastic ordering techniques are used to compare the mean lifetimes of two independent systems under this model, as well as their threshold times and optimal maintenance times.

</details>


### [133] [Hardness of some optimization problems over correlation polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02896)
*Alberto Caprara,Fabio Furini,Claudio Gentile,Leo Liberti,Andrea Lodi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过Karp归约证明了与相关多面体及其对应锥（由所有n×n{0,1}秩一矩阵张成）相关的若干问题的NP困难性，包括：成员判定问题、分解秩问题以及一个“松弛秩”问题（将秩的零范数表达式松弛为ℓ1范数得到）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究相关多面体及其对应锥的计算复杂性，特别是理解这些问题的固有难度。

Method: 使用Karp归约（多项式时间多对一归约）来证明NP困难性。

Result: 证明了成员判定、分解秩以及松弛秩问题均为NP困难。

Conclusion: 与相关多面体锥相关的三个关键计算问题本质上是难以有效求解的（NP困难），这为信号处理和统计应用中的相关算法设计提供了复杂性下界。

Abstract: We prove the \textbf{NP}-hardness, using Karp reductions, of some problems related to the correlation polytope and its corresponding cone, spanned by all of the $n\times n$ rank-one matrices over $\{0,1\}$. The problems are: membership, rank of the decomposition, and a ``relaxed rank'' obtained from relaxing the zero-norm expression for the rank to an $\ell_1$ norm. While membership and rank are natural problems for any matrix cone, the relaxed rank problem occurs in some signal processing and statistical applications.

</details>


### [134] [MultiLRSGA: A method for multi-player differentiable games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03263)
*Katherine Rossella Foglia,Vittorio Colao,Alfio Borzì*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了MultiLRSGA，一种用于可微博弈中计算稳定纳什均衡的多玩家扩展方法，通过低秩辛校正实现局部线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LRSGA从双玩家场景扩展到多玩家场景，解决多玩家博弈中反斜校正不再由单一交叉互动决定，而是由块反斜算子收集所有玩家间成对耦合的挑战。

Method: 基于博弈雅可比矩阵分解为对称和斜对称分量，为每个玩家构建部分梯度雅可比的低秩近似，提取所需块定义近似反斜校正，并引入辛校正衰减动力学的旋转部分。

Result: 在稳定纳什均衡的标准局部假设下，证明了方法的局部线性收敛性。关键引理控制了多玩家设置下精确反斜校正与其割线近似之间的距离。

Conclusion: MultiLRSGA保留了低秩辛校正的计算优势，适用于具有显式收益且超过两个智能体的可微博弈的数值验证。

Abstract: We propose MultiLRSGA, an $h$-player extension of LRSGA for the computation of stable Nash equilibria in differentiable games. The method originates from the decomposition of the game Jacobian into symmetric and antisymmetric components, which motivates symplectic corrections designed to attenuate the rotational part of the dynamics. In the two-player setting, LRSGA replaces mixed second-order blocks with low-rank secant approximations. The passage to the multi-player case, however, is not a mere blockwise reformulation: the antisymmetric correction is no longer determined by a single pair of cross-interactions, but by a block antisymmetric operator collecting all pairwise couplings among the players. On this basis, we formulate MultiLRSGA by constructing, for each player, a low-rank approximation of the Jacobian of the partial gradient and extracting from it the blocks required to define an approximate antisymmetric correction. Under standard local assumptions around a stable Nash equilibrium, we prove local linear convergence of the method. The key technical ingredient is a lemma controlling the distance between the exact antisymmetric correction and its secant approximation in the $h$-player setting, thereby extending to the multi-player framework the convergence mechanism previously available for LRSGA. The proposed formulation preserves the computational advantages of low-rank symplectic corrections and is naturally suited to numerical validation on differentiable games with explicit payoffs and more than two agents.

</details>


### [135] [A Quadratic-Approximation-Based Stochastic Approximation Method for Weakly Convex Stochastic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03400)
*Yule Zhang,Benqi Liu,Xiantao Xiao,Liwei Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的随机近似算法PMQSopt，用于求解涉及期望值函数的弱凸随机优化问题。该算法结合了近端乘子法和原始问题的二次逼近。在弱凸性和严格可行点存在这两个温和条件下，建立了算法在梯度范数、约束违反和互补性违反三个KKT条件度量下的收敛率，期望收敛率为O(T^{-1/4})，并以高概率给出了相应的概率界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机优化算法通常要求问题函数具有强凸性或凸性，而许多实际问题（如机器学习中的非凸损失函数）仅满足弱凸性。本文旨在为弱凸随机优化问题设计一种可有效求解且具有理论保证的算法，并建立其样本复杂度与收敛性分析。

Method: 将近端乘子法（proximal method of multipliers）与原始随机问题的二次近似（quadratic approximation）相结合，构造PMQSopt算法。该算法本质上是一种序列强凸规划方法，在每次迭代中求解一个强凸子问题。

Result: (1) 在期望意义上，梯度平方范数、约束违反和互补性违反均在T次迭代后达到O(T^{-1/4})的收敛率；(2) 高概率下，梯度满足O(T^{-1/8})界（概率≥1-1/T^{2/3}），约束违反和互补性违反满足O(T^{-1/4})界（概率分别≥1-2/T^{2/3}和≥1-3/T^{2/3}）。

Conclusion: PMQSopt是一种可实现的序列强凸规划方法，在弱凸性和严格可行点存在条件下具有理论保证，数值实验验证了其实际有效性。

Abstract: We propose a novel stochastic approximation algorithm, termed PMQSopt, for solving weakly convex stochastic optimization problems involving expectation-valued functions. The algorithm is constructed by integrating the proximal method of multipliers with quadratic approximations of the original stochastic problem. We analyze the sample complexity of PMQSopt in terms of the total number of stochastic gradient evaluations required. The convergence of the algorithm is characterized by three metrics associated with the $ε$-KKT conditions: the average squared norm of the gradient of the Moreau envelope of the Lagrangian, the average constraint violation, and the average complementarity violation. For each of these metrics, we establish an expected convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ after $T$ iterations. Furthermore, we show that with probability at least $1-1/T^{2/3}$, the gradient of the Lagrangian satisfies an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/8})$ bound; with probability at least $1-2/T^{2/3}$, the constraint violation achieves an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ bound; and with probability at least $1-3/T^{2/3}$, the complementarity violation attains an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ bound. All results are established under two mild conditions: (i) weak convexity of all problem functions, and (ii) the existence of a strictly feasible point. The proposed PMQSopt algorithm is a sequentially strongly convex programming method that is readily implementable. Numerical experiments illustrate its practical performance.

</details>


### [136] [A Proximal Augmented Lagrangian Method Based on Quadratic Approximations for Weakly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03415)
*Yule Zhang,Benqi Liu,Xiantao Xiao,Liwei Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出QPALM算法，一种基于二次近似的近端增广拉格朗日方法，用于求解具有弱凸目标函数和约束函数的非线性规划问题。该算法在近端拉格朗日框架中融入目标函数和约束函数的二次近似，证明其非渐近收敛率为O(T^{-1/3})，并通过三个ε-KKT条件指标刻画收敛性。初步数值实验验证了其实用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性规划方法对弱凸问题的理论保证不足，尤其是非渐近收敛率分析缺乏。作者旨在设计一种适用于弱凸目标函数和约束函数的可实现算法，并提供严格的收敛性理论保证。

Method: 将目标函数和约束函数的二次近似融入近端拉格朗日框架，构建顺序强凸规划方法。通过Moreau包络梯度、平均约束违反度和平均互补违反度三个ε-KKT指标分析收敛性。

Result: 三个ε-KKT条件指标均以O(T^{-1/3})的速率收敛；数值实验验证了方法的实际效率。

Conclusion: QPALM在弱凸问题假设和严格可行点存在的温和条件下，实现了可证明的非渐近收敛率，且方法易于实现，为弱凸非线性规划提供了理论保障与实用工具。

Abstract: This paper proposes QPALM, a proximal augmented Lagrangian method based on quadratic approximations, for solving nonlinear programming problems with weakly convex objective and constraint functions. The algorithm is constructed by incorporating quadratic approximations of both the objective and constraint functions into a proximal Lagrangian framework. We establish its non-asymptotic convergence rate in terms of the total number of subproblems solved. The convergence of QPALM is characterized by three metrics associated with the $\varepsilon$-KKT conditions: the squared norm of the gradient of the Moreau envelope of the Lagrangian, the average constraint violation, and the average complementarity violation. All three metrics are shown to converge at a rate of $O(T^{-1/3})$ after $T$ iterations. Preliminary numerical results demonstrate the practical efficiency of the proposed method. These results are established under two mild conditions: (i) weak convexity of all problem functions, and (ii) the existence of a strictly feasible point. The proposed QPALM is a sequentially strongly convex programming method that is readily implementable.

</details>


### [137] [Promoting Fair Online Resource Allocation with Indivisible Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03436)
*Igor Averbakh,Hongyi Jiang,Samitha Samaranayake,Akang Wang,Jianghua Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线不可分割资源分配中的公平性问题，提出了FORA-IU问题框架，设计了不同到达假设下的在线策略，证明了平稳性分布能严格提升公平性边界，且部分满足是实现最优公平的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀缺不可分割资源在不确定环境下分配给多样化群体时，传统效率导向方法往往忽视边缘群体。本文旨在探索在不同分布和结构假设下，在线不可分割资源分配能实现何种公平性保障。

Method: 基于预期填充率（FE-FR-beta）的公平准则，设计校准接受概率与剩余预算的在线策略，分析任意时变和固定到达两种情形；提出随机循环块（RCB）算法处理平稳到达情形，并研究全有或全无分配策略的限制。

Result: 任意时变到达下，策略达到最优通用公平保障1/(1+R_beta)；时间不变到达下，RCB达到精确有限时域保障[1-(1-R_beta/T)^T]/R_beta，至少为(1-e^{-R_beta})/R_beta；全有或全无策略无法匹配这些保障。

Conclusion: 分布平稳性严格改善公平边界，部分满足是在线不可分割资源分配中实现最优公平的必要条件。

Abstract: Allocating scarce, indivisible resources to diverse groups under uncertainty is a central challenge in operations research, where efficiency-focused methods often underserve marginalized populations. We study the Fair Online Resource Allocation with Indivisible Units (FORA-IU) problem, in which an unpredictable sequence of demands must be served from a strictly fixed inventory, and ask what fairness guarantees are achievable under different distributional and structural assumptions.
  We adopt a fairness criterion based on the expected filling ratio (FE-FR-beta), which balances each group's expected allocation against its expected demand and priority weight. We design online policies that calibrate acceptance probabilities to the remaining budget, analyze both arbitrary time-varying and stationary arrivals, introduce the Random Cyclic Blocks (RCB) algorithm tailored to the stationary case, and study the effect of restricting policies to all-or-nothing allocations.
  For arbitrary time-varying arrivals, our policy achieves the optimal universal fairness guarantee of 1/(1+R_beta), where R_beta denotes the priority-weighted system load. For time-invariant arrivals, RCB achieves the exact finite-horizon guarantee [1-(1-R_beta/T)^T]/R_beta, which is at least (1-e^{-R_beta})/R_beta and is also tight. We further show that all-or-nothing allocation policies cannot match these guarantees.
  These findings demonstrate that distributional stationarity strictly improves the fairness frontier, and that partial fulfillment is a necessary condition for attaining optimal fairness in online indivisible resource allocation.

</details>


### [138] [Quantitative Convergence of Proximal Splitting Iterations in Uniformly Convex Metric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03484)
*D. Russell Luke,Mahshid Mirhashemi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出了在度量空间上最小化函数和的近端分裂算法迭代的定量收敛充分条件，该理论不要求函数有公共最小值，也不要求近端参数或步长趋于零，并在一类CAT(κ)空间及Hadamard空间上展示了应用实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近端分裂算法的收敛理论通常要求函数有公共最小值、近端参数或步长趋于零，且多局限于Hilbert空间。作者旨在建立更一般的收敛框架，放宽这些限制条件，使其适用于更广泛的度量空间（如p-一致凸且曲率上有界的空间）。

Method: 在一般p-一致凸且曲率上有界的度量空间上建立定量收敛的充分条件，通过特殊的结构假设（如近端映射的收缩性质）推导迭代的收敛率。然后作为特例将主定理推广到Hadamard空间（此时许多一般性假设可省略），并在SPD矩阵空间（仿射不变度量）和球面（标准测地度量）上验证理论的有效性。

Result: 给出了近端分裂算法在p-一致凸且曲率上有界空间上的定量收敛充分条件，并在Hadamard空间上得到了更简洁的收敛保证。在SPD矩阵空间（计算Fréchet均值）和球面几何上的实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个更通用的近端分裂算法收敛分析框架，显著放宽了已有理论的条件限制（不要求公共最小值、不要求步长/参数衰减），并已在两类重要的非线性空间上得到验证。

Abstract: We provide sufficient conditions for quantitative convergence of the iterates of proximal splitting algorithms for minimizing a sum of functions on a metric space. The theory does not assume that the functions have common minima, nor does it require vanishing proximal parameters or step sizes. Our results are stated for general $p$-uniformly convex spaces with curvature bounded above, and a corollary specializes the main theorem to Hadamard spaces, where many assumptions for the more general setting can be dropped. The theory is demonstrated with computation of Fréchet means in the space of SPD matrices with the affine invariant metric (a Hadamard space) and the sphere with the usual geodesic metric (a CAT($κ$) metric space).

</details>


### [139] [One-Dimensional Nonnegative Spline Smoothing via Convex Semi-Infinite Programming with a Cutting-Plane Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03711)
*Hiroki Arai,Daichi Kitahara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种切割平面（CP）方法，通过迭代求解二次规划（QP）子问题，有效解决了1D非负样条平滑中的凸半无限规划（CSIP）问题。该方法相比传统的基于充分条件的QP方法和基于局部约简的序列二次规划（LRSQP）方法，在保证收敛性的同时提高了求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非负样条平滑使用充分条件（无穷多个线性不等式的子集）来保证非负性，虽然可通过QP快速求解，但可能得到次优解。而直接处理无穷不等式约束的CSIP问题虽能获得精确解，但现有LRSQP算法因多次近似导致收敛性能下降。因此需要一种既能精确求解又能快速收敛的新方法。

Method: 将一维非负样条平滑建模为CSIP问题；初始化时使用标准样条平滑得到初始解；迭代过程中：对每个多项式段求最小化（低次多项式用闭式解，高次用数值解），若最小值为负，则将该极小值点作为约束加入问题；通过QP快速求解更新后的约束问题；重复直至所有多项式段最小值非负。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提CP方法相比传统充分条件QP方法和MATLAB LRSQP算法，在收敛性和解的质量上均有优势。

Conclusion: 所提切割平面方法能够高效、精确地求解非负样条平滑的CSIP问题，且收敛性得到理论保证，是一种优于传统方法的实用算法。

Abstract: Spline functions are smooth piecewise polynomials widely used for interpolation and smoothing, and nonnegative spline smoothing is also studied for nonnegative data. Previous research used sufficient conditions for the nonnegativity of spline functions because necessary and sufficient conditions for the nonnegativity are infinitely many linear inequalities, which are difficult to handle in optimization algorithms. This conventional method quickly computes a nonnegative spline function via quadratic programming (QP), but the optimal solution may be slightly degraded by using the sufficient condition. In this paper, we express 1D nonnegative spline smoothing as a convex semi-infinite programming (CSIP) problem that directly deals with infinite inequality constraints. As optimization algorithms for general SIP problems, local-reduction-based sequential quadratic programming (LRSQP) methods are used, but their convergence performance deteriorates for certain problems due to multiple approximations during updates. To quickly solve the CSIP problem, we propose a cutting-plane (CP) method. In the proposed method, after giving an initial solution by the standard spline smoothing, we find the minimizer of each polynomial piece by using the closed-form solution for a low-degree polynomial or a numerical solution for a high-degree polynomial. If the minimum value is negative, then such minimizer is added into the constraint of the problem to guarantee the nonnegativity. This constrained problem is quickly solved via QP, and we find the minimizer of each polynomial piece again. We repeat these procedures until there are no negative minimum values. The proposed method guarantees convergence to the original CSIP solution, and its effectiveness is demonstrated in numerical experiments by comparison to the conventional methods, QP under the sufficient condition and CSIP using the MATLAB LRSQP algorithm.

</details>


### [140] [Global exponential stabilization of a force- and torque-actuated unicycle by flexible-step MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03726)
*Ala Kolsi,Christian Ebenbauer,Bahman Gharesifard,Raik Suttner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于柔性步长MPC的全局指数镇定方法，适用于离散时间力-力矩驱动独轮车模型，无需特定终端代价或约束设计，通过广义离散时间控制Lyapunov函数保证稳定性，并证明了动态独轮车模型在经典意义下无法实现全局指数稳定，但所提方法能在稍弱意义下达到最优稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对离散时间力-力矩驱动独轮车模型的全局指数镇定问题，现有MPC方法通常依赖终端代价函数或终端约束的设计，缺乏灵活步长与状态相关权重的理论框架。

Method: 扩展了柔性步长MPC方法，提出首个具有状态相关权重的柔性步长MPC协议，利用广义离散时间控制Lyapunov函数替代传统终端代价与约束，建立全局指数镇定的新理论框架，并针对动态独轮车模型分析了稳定性的可实现性。

Result: 证明了动态（二阶）独轮车模型在经典意义下无法实现全局指数稳定，但所提柔性步长MPC方法能在稍弱意义下达到该模型最优的全局指数稳定性；提供了参数选择的显式规则以保证可行性与稳定性；数值实验验证了该方法在连续时间力矩驱动独轮车上的有效性。

Conclusion: 柔性步长MPC方法为非线性离散时间控制系统提供了一种无需终端代价/约束的全局指数镇定新框架，并在独轮车模型上取得了理论最优的稳定性结果。

Abstract: We study the problem of global exponential stabilization of a force- and torque-controlled unicycle model in discrete time. To this end, we extend a recently introduced approach to model predictive control (MPC) in which a flexible number of inputs is implemented in every iteration. We present the first flexible-step MPC protocol with state-dependent weights for average descent. Notably, the proposed method relies neither on a suitable design of running or terminal cost functions nor on a suitable choice of terminal constraints. Instead, stability is guaranteed through a generalized discrete-time control Lyapunov function. We establish a new theoretical framework for global exponential stabilization of general nonlinear discrete-time control systems by flexible-step MPC. The obtained results go beyond the unicycle example. However, given the importance of the unicycle dynamics, we make that a focal point of our work. For the particular case of the dynamic (second-order) unicycle model, we show that global exponential stability cannot be attained in the classical sense, but in a slightly weaker sense. The proposed flexible-step MPC method is shown to induce the best possible notion of global exponential stability for this model. We provide explicit rules for the choice of parameters, which guarantee feasibility and global exponential stability. Our numerical simulations show that the discrete MPC method also works very well in applications to a continuous-time torque-actuated unicycle.

</details>


### [141] [Exact and Evolutionary Algorithms for Sequential Multi-Objective Transmission Topology Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03753)
*Job Groeneveld,Miguel Muñoz,Jan Viebahn,Alessandro Zocca*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对日前输电拓扑规划与阻塞管理问题，提出了精确枚举法（块算法）和基于NSGA-III的多目标进化算法。块算法利用策略的时间块结构在多项式时间内枚举完整Pareto前沿，能在三分钟内计算高峰时段完整前沿；进化算法虽收敛但无法恢复精确前沿。块算法既可作实际决策支持工具，也可作为未来启发式与学习方法的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日前输电拓扑规划与阻塞管理是一个序贯多目标优化问题，现有方法难以同时考虑TSO实际决策中的多个目标（N-1安全下的最坏线路负载、拓扑深度、开关动作次数、非参考拓扑持续时间），且缺乏可计算完整Pareto前沿的精确方法作为基准。

Method: 将问题建模为序贯多目标优化问题，包含四个目标函数。提出块算法（Block Algorithm），利用可行策略的时间块结构枚举完整Pareto前沿；提出基于NSGA-III的多目标进化算法，采用结构引导初始化和问题特定变异算子。使用TenneT荷兰高压电网实际运营数据进行验证。

Result: 块算法能在三分钟内计算高度拥堵日的完整Pareto前沿；进化算法收敛于精确前沿附近但无法完全恢复；块算法的评估次数随规划时域呈多项式增长。

Conclusion: 块算法既是一个实用的决策支持工具，也为该类问题的未来启发式和学习方法提供了精确基准。

Abstract: We address day-ahead transmission topology planning and congestion management as a sequential, multi-objective optimization problem and develop two complementary algorithms for it: an exact enumeration method and a tailored evolutionary heuristic. The problem is formulated with four operational objectives reflecting real TSO decision criteria: worst-case line loading under $N-1$ security, topological depth, number of switching actions, and time spent in non-reference topologies, over a 24-hour horizon. We introduce the block algorithm, an exact method that exploits the temporal block structure of feasible strategies to enumerate the complete Pareto front; for fixed operational bounds on depth and switch count, its evaluation count grows polynomially with the planning horizon. We complement it with a multi-objective evolutionary algorithm based on NSGA-III, with structure-guided initialization and problem-specific variation operators tailored to the topology-planning structure. Using real operational data from the Dutch high-voltage grid operated by TenneT TSO, we show that the block algorithm computes the full Pareto front for a highly congested day in under three minutes, and that the evolutionary algorithm converges toward but does not recover the exact front. The block algorithm thus provides both a practical decision-support tool and a ground-truth benchmark for future heuristic and learning-based methods on this problem class.

</details>


### [142] [Learning Dominant States in Elementary Resource Constrained Shortest Path Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03760)
*Saverio Basso,Matteo Salani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究利用机器学习识别动态规划算法中的有前途状态，聚焦于基本资源约束最短路径问题。通过PathWyse库求解41个实例，收集数亿标签，设计常数额外开销的特征构建数据集，发现连续松弛间的显著模式，提出归一化方法并应用监督学习区分支配状态。在完整标签集上效果显著，但在部分数据集上跨实例泛化能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索机器学习能否用于识别动态规划算法中的有前途状态，以提升算法效率，特别是在基本资源约束最短路径问题中。

Method: 求解41个ERCSPP实例，收集所有生成标签和插入数据池的标签；设计常数额外开销的特征；构建两个数据集G和I；使用机器学习工具探索数据模式；提出归一化方法；应用监督学习区分支配状态。

Result: 在数据集G上方法有效；在数据集I上，同实例内表现良好，但跨未见实例时性能下降。

Conclusion: 该工作为数据驱动动态规划算法的开发开辟了新视角，但跨实例泛化能力仍需改进。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate whether machine learning can be leveraged to identify promising states in dynamic programming algorithms, focusing on Elementary Resource Constrained Shortest Path Problems (ERCSPP). More in detail, we solved 41 single resource instances from SPPRCLIB using iterative relaxation techniques through the PathWyse library, systematically collecting all generated states (i.e. labels). We designed ad-hoc features computable in constant time and constructed two datasets: one containing all generated labels (G) and another with only those inserted into data pools (I), totaling several hundred million labels. Machine learning tools are then employed to explore these datasets, revealing significant patterns between successive relaxations. Leveraging these insights, we propose a normalization approach and apply supervised learning techniques to distinguish dominating states, both within subsequent relaxations of the same problem and in previously unseen instances. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of this approach on Dataset G, while for Dataset I, performance varies, showing strong results within the same instance but declining for unseen ones. Overall, these findings open new perspectives for the development of data-driven dynamic programming algorithms.

</details>


### [143] [On the Induced Norms of Matrices and Grothendieck problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03772)
*Lan V. Truong,M. H. Duong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种纯分析框架，能够精确计算诱导矩阵范数 $\|\bA\|_{q \to r}$ 对于所有 $q, r \ge 1$ 的取值，并应用于多类重要矩阵，同时通过该范数与Grothendieck问题的直接联系，给出了这些问题的精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诱导矩阵范数 $\|\bA\|_{q \to r}$ 的精确值长期以来仅在少数经典情形下已知，其变分定义的高度非凸性质使得精确求解非常困难，现有工作仅能提供数值估计或解析界，缺乏精确公式。

Method: 提出纯分析框架，通过建立诱导范数与Grothendieck问题之间的直接联系，为多类重要矩阵精确计算 $\|\bA\|_{q \to r}$ 对所有 $q, r \ge 1$ 的取值。

Result: 成功确定了多类重要矩阵的诱导范数精确值，并同时给出了对应Grothendieck问题的精确解。

Conclusion: 该分析框架填补了诱导矩阵范数精确计算的理论空白，并揭示了其与Grothendieck问题之间的内在联系。

Abstract: We study the induced matrix norm $\|\bA\|_{q \to r}$, whose exact value has been known only in a few classical cases. Determining this norm has long been regarded as difficult due to the highly non-convex nature of its variational definition. Existing works offer numerical estimates or analytic bounds but no exact formula. In this paper we present a purely analytic framework that determines $\|\bA\|_{q \to r}$ exactly for all $q, r \ge 1$ for several classes of important matrices. For these matrices, using a direct connection between the induced norms and Grothendieck problems, our results also simultaneously provide exact values for the later.

</details>


### [144] [The Distributionally Robust Cyclic Inventory Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03785)
*Menglei Jia,Albert H. Schrotenboer,Ahmadreza Marandi,Feng Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限循环时间域下的循环库存路径问题，提出了一个分布鲁棒优化模型，通过最坏情况期望成本最小化和分布鲁棒机会约束保障服务可靠性，并设计了嵌套分支定价算法进行求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有库存路径问题（IRP）研究多假设需求分布已知，但实际中需求分布常不确定。为应对需求分布的不确定性，本文引入分布鲁棒优化方法，在仅知需求矩信息的情况下做出鲁棒的路径与补货决策。

Method: 构建基于矩模糊集的分布鲁棒优化模型；证明最坏情况期望库存成本由多点分布实现，可通过线性规划后验识别；将分布鲁棒机会约束转化为近等价的确定性形式；设计嵌套分支定价框架，第一层将零售商分簇，第二层处理簇内路径与补货决策。

Result: 在合成数据及上汽大众真实数据上的计算实验验证了所提方法的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: 分布鲁棒优化结合嵌套分支定价能够有效求解需求分布不确定下的循环库存路径问题，实现成本与服务可靠性的平衡。

Abstract: We study the cyclic inventory routing problem that involves joint decisions on vehicle routing and inventory replenishment on an infinite, cyclic horizon. It considers a single warehouse and a set of geographically dispersed retailers. We model retailer demand as random variables with uncertain distributions belonging to a moment-based ambiguity set. We develop a distributionally robust optimization formulation that minimizes the worst-case expected cost over the ambiguity set, while ensuring service reliability through a distributionally robust chance constraint. Our main results are that we prove that the worst-case expected inventory cost is attained under a multi-point distribution, which can be identified a posteriori via linear programming, and that the distributionally robust chance constraint can be reformulated into near-equivalent deterministic forms. This yields a deterministic reformulation of the original problem. To solve it, we design a nested branch-and-price framework, in which the first level partitions retailers into clusters, and the second level concerns routing and replenishment decisions within each cluster. Computational experiments on both synthetic instances and real-world data from SAIC Volkswagen Automobile Co., Ltd. demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [145] [Ball-proximal point method on a Hadamard Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03815)
*F. Babu,O. P. Ferreira,L. F. Prudente,Jen-Chih Yao,Xiaopeng Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Hadamard流形上提出并分析了一种黎曼球面邻近点方法（RB-PPM），该方法通过在当前迭代点的度量球内最小化目标函数来求解测地凸优化问题，建立了收敛性理论，包括拟Fejér单调性、有限终止性、线性收敛率以及渐近二分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将欧氏空间中的球面邻近点（broximal）思想推广到Hadamard流形上，用于求解测地凸函数的极小化问题，提供一种几何感知的、基于球面的黎曼邻近点方法替代方案。

Method: 提出黎曼球面邻近点方法（RB-PPM），其基本步骤是在当前迭代点的度量球内最小化目标函数；引入黎曼broximal映射，证明其在Hadamard流形上存在唯一性，并推导涉及标量参数和黎曼次微分的KKT型刻画。

Result: 证明了当当前球不包含极小点时，迭代点到解集的平方距离严格递减；建立了拟Fejér单调性、常数半径下的有限终止性以及目标值的乘积形式线性衰减；给出了子梯度范数和函数值的非渐近复杂度界（包括常数半径下的线性率）；建立了渐近二分性：若半径和发散则目标值收敛到最优值，且当解集非空时整个迭代序列收敛到一个极小点。

Conclusion: RB-PPM提供了经典黎曼邻近点方法的一种几何感知的、基于球面的模拟方案，并在Hadamard流形上建立了完整的收敛理论。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing a proper, lower semicontinuous, geodesically convex function on a Hadamard manifold. Building on ball-proximal (broximal) ideas in the Euclidean setting, viewed as an abstract proximal-type algorithm, we propose and analyze a Riemannian ball-proximal point method (RB-PPM) whose basic step consists of minimizing the objective function over a metric ball centred at the current iterate. We first introduce the Riemannian broximal map, prove existence and uniqueness of broximal points on Hadamard manifolds, and derive a KKT-type characterization involving a scalar parameter and the Riemannian subdifferential. We then show that RB-PPM enjoys a strict decrease of the squared distance to the solution set whenever the current ball does not contain a minimizer. This leads to quasi-Fejér monotonicity, finite termination for constant radii, and a product-form linear decay of the objective values up to the hitting time of the solution set. We also obtain nonasymptotic complexity bounds for the norms of suitable subgradients and for the function values, including a linear rate in the number of iterations under constant radii. Finally, we establish an asymptotic dichotomy, if the sum of the radii diverges, then the objective values converge to the optimal value, and, when the solution set is nonempty, the entire sequence of iterates converges to a minimizer. The resulting scheme provides a geometry-aware, ball-based analog of classical Riemannian proximal point methods.

</details>


### [146] [Extended-variable relaxations for the constrained generalized maximum-entropy sampling problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03959)
*Gabriel Ponte,Kurt Anstreicher,Marcia Fampa,Jon Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对限制性广义最大熵抽样问题（CGMESP），提出了新颖的非凸扩展变量公式及相应的连续松弛方法，建立了不同上界之间的关系，并提出了广义缩放技术以改进边界，最后通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CGMESP是最大熵抽样问题（MESP）和二元D-最优性问题的自然推广，已在统计设计理论中存在超过25年，且在PCA选择问题中有重要应用动机。

Method: 提出非凸扩展变量公式；基于此推导非凸及凸连续松弛；建立文献中已有上界与新上界之间的关系；提出广义缩放技术以改进边界；比较两种分支定界中的自然分支策略。

Result: 建立了不同上界之间的多种关系；验证了约束连接自然变量与扩展变量的松弛行为；提出了有效的边界改进技术；确定了更优的分支策略。

Conclusion: 本文的方法为CGMESP提供了新的有效松弛和边界改进技术，数值实验表明这些方法具有实用价值。

Abstract: The constrained generalized maximum-entropy sampling problem (CGMESP) is to select an order-s principal submatrix from an order-n covariance matrix, subject to some linear side constraints, so as to maximize the product of its t greatest eigenvalues, 0 < t <= s <n. GMESP refers to the version with no side constraints. Introduced more than 25 years ago, CGMESP is a natural generalization of two fundamental problems in statistical design theory: (i) constrained maximum-entropy sampling problem (CMESP); (ii) binary D-optimality (D-Opt). In the general case, it can be motivated by a selection problem in the context of principal component analysis (PCA).
  We present novel non-convex extended variable formulations for CGMESP. Using these formulations as points of departure, we present, first non-convex and then convex, continuous relaxations for CGMESP. We demonstrate many relations between different upper bounds for CGMESP, including upper bounds from the literature and our new upper bounds. We investigate the behavior of our relaxations related to the constraints linking the natural variables with the extended variables. We propose and investigate a generalized scaling technique for bound improvement. In the context of branch-and-bound, we determine the better of two natural branching techniques for fixing variables to zero. Finally, we present numerical experiments illustrating the value of our methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [CreativityBench: Evaluating Agent Creative Reasoning via Affordance-Based Tool Repurposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02910)
*Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiayu Liu,Bingxiang He,Jeonghwan Kim,Jiateng Liu,Bingxuan Li,Aditi Tiwari,Dwip Dalal,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Mahdi Namazifar,Yunzhu Li,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CreativityBench基准，用于评估大语言模型的创造性工具使用能力。基于包含4K实体和15万+功能注释的大规模功能知识库，生成了1.4万个需要发现非显而易见物理可行解决方案的任务。评估10个SOTA模型发现：模型能选择合理物体，但无法识别正确部件及其功能机制；模型规模扩展收益迅速饱和；强推理能力无法迁移到创造性功能发现；思维链等策略提升有限。结论：创造性工具使用仍是当前模型的主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理和交互任务上表现强劲，但其创造性问题解决能力（尤其是创造性工具使用——通过推理物体的功能属性而非传统用法来重新利用现有物体）仍未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 构建大规模功能知识库（4K实体、15万+功能注释），连接对象、部件、属性和可行用途；2) 基于该知识库生成1.4万个需要发现非显而易见但物理可行解决方案的约束任务；3) 在10个SOTA LLM（包括闭源和开源）上评估。

Result: 模型能选择合理物体，但无法识别正确部件、其功能及所需物理机制；模型规模扩展收益迅速饱和；强通用推理能力无法可靠迁移到创造性功能发现；Chain-of-Thought等推理策略提升有限。

Conclusion: 创造性工具使用仍是当前模型的主要挑战，CreativityBench为研究智能的这一缺失维度提供了有用测试平台，对未来智能体的规划和推理模块设计具有潜在影响。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have led to strong performance on reasoning and environment-interaction tasks, yet their ability for creative problem-solving remains underexplored. We study this capability through the lens of creative tool use, where a model repurposes available objects by reasoning about their affordances and attributes rather than relying on canonical usage. As a first step, we introduce CreativityBench, a benchmark for evaluating affordance-based creativity in LLMs. To this end, we build a large-scale affordance knowledge base (KB) with 4K entities and 150K+ affordance annotations, explicitly linking objects, parts, attributes, and actionable uses. Building on this KB, we generate 14K grounded tasks that require identifying non-obvious yet physically plausible solutions under constraints. Evaluations across 10 state-of-the-art LLMs, including closed and open-source models, show that models can often select a plausible object, but fail to identify the correct parts, their affordances, and the underlying physical mechanism needed to solve the task, leading to a significant drop in performance. Furthermore, improvements from model scaling quickly saturate, strong general reasoning does not reliably translate to creative affordance discovery, and common inference-time strategies such as Chain-of-Thought yield limited gains. These results suggest that creative tool use remains a major challenge for current models, and that CreativityBench provides a useful testbed for studying this missing dimension of intelligence, with potential implications for planning and reasoning modules in future agents.

</details>
